Beta 14.1.1 - 28/07/2024
--------------------------------------

IMPORTANT: Tianxia has been rebased off of CleanSlate; CleanSlate 1.0 Beta 6.1 [EZKM] (https://github.com/ck2plus/CleanSlate/releases/tag/1.0-beta6.1) MUST be active for Tianxia to run.
IMPORTANT: Any old Tianxia.mod file MUST be replaced with the new one; the new version contains important changes, such as actually setting up CleanSlate as a dependency.
IMPORTANT: Delete any old Tianxia directory; don't just overwrite it with the new one.
IMPORTANT: Pre-14.1.0 saves will not work due to traits being added.
IMPORTANT: Random/Shattered world and the EU4 Converter are not supported.
IMPORTANT: Start dates after 1127.4.20 remain unsupported; it's possible to start in them, but they're not expected to be in a good spot, and are not a priority to fix.

Religion features:
- Made it clear that Pyramid Creation is permissible for Chinese-/Japanese-style government reformations.
- Made it clear that Autonomous Leadership is permissible for Chinese-/Japanese-style government reformations (obviously excepting in the case you wish to go Divine Imperial).
- Peacecful Nature now makes the AI willing to convert provinces if sufficiently zealous (similar to Jain).
- Warlike Nature now makes the AI willing to convert other religion group provincess if sufficiently zealous (as opposed to just converting inside its own religion group); there's no good reason not to have the AI do that.
- Divine Ancestry now also has religion head defence; that brings it closer to Shinto.

Combat tactics:
- Awesome Advance is no longer something even the poorest commanders are likely to pull off.

Events:
- Devil Worshippers are now correctly only caught by Court Chaplains if accessible/etc., as opposed to only when inaccessible/etc.
- Hwarang flavour events no longer have inverted accessibility checks in some places.
- Fixed only inaccessible/etc. characters getting to look for Vijaya; they are instead correctly ineligible.
- Kitsune now only teach accessible/etc. children, as opposed to only inaccessible/etc. children.
- Fixed Court Alchemists only being asked to brew harmful potions for inaccessible/etc. characters; inverted blocker.
- Picking "Actually, I don't think I will!" in the first event when gathering ingredients will now fail the related mission for the Hermetics and the RAS.
- AI characters bypassing RAS missions now rarely gain a random ingredient.
- LNY hosts now only get one possible boon to ask of Chinese dragons, seeing as the event otherwise looks quite weird.
- Fixed hospital charity/food event giving you money.
- Seeing as their founding event speaks of the reconquest of Calatrava and that Catholics have plenty of holy orders without them, the Knights of Calatrava will no longer spawn if Calatrava hasn't actually been properly reconquered by the Catholics (or a Catholic heresy that has become mainstream).
- LNY hosts will no longer also get told they're welcoming their guests to a vanilla feast.
- Grandchildren with an appropriate gender (that are children of a child with an appropriate gender) should now inherit Japanese government traits.
- Fraternal Seniority should now be preserved on inheritance if the heir is eligible for the law.
- Fixed edge case with enlisted Ronin not standing down.
- The WotRS will no longer tell you to wage a War of Honor on someone with a regent.
- Hellenic temple dedication is now more restrictive with Agnatic or Enatic Clans; why would you dedicate a temple to an evil god(dess)?
- "The Messenger" is now preferably within the would-be-DW's diplo range, seeing as that makes later seduction easier.
- Tweaked conditions for HF battle nicknames; some were a bit nonsensical.
- Hanami invitations no longer check for the Chuseok being ongoing.
- Rulers no longer get to make landed characters into cannibals randomly at feasts; only courtiers.
- AI Taoists now also get notified that the future looks pretty terrible when the Black Death has come.
- Fixed WotRS mentors not getting the proper opinion modifier towards students that finished their training.
- WotRS mentor events now make use of event targets, which should reduce the chances of scopes getting messed up.
- Culture spread events no longer operate according to weird rules for naval adjacencies.
- Characters that are not in a secret society will no longer go "I should lay low for a while..." if a fellow society member is caught by a court chaplain.
- RAS and Hermetic lab infiltrators no longer get two different "I can brew a potion of dreamless sleep!" options.
- Wandering Genius physicians now takes culture and religion from a random province, as opposed to randomizing a culture yet somehow always belonging to your religion.
- Wandering Genius physicians are now Chaste rather than Celibate.
- Assorted WotRS flavour can no longer fire for characters that are inaccessible/incapable/imprisoned.
- Vassals will no longer inform you that *apparently* you demanded Gavelkind/an end to a war without you knowing; the related events now correctly check for an AI liege.
- AI lieges will no longer care to demand Gavelkind from vassals that only have one county (unless they have secondary baronies).
- Non-tribal/-nomadic rulers will now offer incense as a more expensive offering than food when engaging in ancestor worship (i.e. the options' costs and effects have been swapped for these rulers).
- Increased RAS hospital/hospital building discount in events to 25 % of the (base) money cost + 100 % of the (base) money cost in society currency.
- You can no longer loot the artefacts of your own liege(s) during a war (unless at war with them).
- Mass Conversion no longer converts third party religion provinces.
- Members of the RAS and the Hwarang will no longer randomly convert, seeing as that might make them ineligible for their societies.
- WotRS members will no longer secretly convert if flagged to never convert.
- The AI now gets full credit for recovering the Pentarchy seats, as opposed to the player potnetially getting the credit for past AI achievements.
- All Orthodox heresies capable of Mending the Great Schism now also get credit for recovering the Pentarchy seats.
- The mother of the future Demon Spawn now gains a temporary health boost while pregnant.
- The Demon Spawn now gets a health boost when born.
- Aphrodisiacs and Fertility Potions now trigger the correct "I will not pry into the matter" event if they were requested by the receiver.
- The world should once again be informed if a CI ruler recreates China by decision.
- CI rulers recreating China by decision should once again get a proper bloodline (unless they already have created one).
- The Sohei now properly get their temples and become vassals of the Tenno when they spawn dynamically.
- The Demon Spawn no longer gets a nanny if they're not Christian.
- Dogmatic pagans are now consistently blocked from becoming Syncretic everywhere.
- Peaceful pagans are now consistently blocked from becoming Militant everywhere.
- Nomads no longer get to baptize their children, seeing as their concept of religion generally is pretty fluid.
- JD book writing will no longer progress if you are inaccessible/incapable/imprisoned.
- JD book writing can now finish up even if the writer has become unlanded.
- The AI should now consistently be blocked from ranking up in societies if inaccessible/incapable/imprisoned, excepting automatic "You became the grand master!" rank ups.
- You should no longer have an Eternal Life mystic show up and then get a delayed "The attempt to find a mystic failed!" event.
- Interest group infighting should now only fire for people that can challenge someone, seeing as they otherwise lose prestige due to "choosing" not to challenge someone.
- Random same society friend/lover/rival events now require both parties to not be inaccessible/incapable/imprisoned.
- The Saoshyant is now scripted to never convert in AI hands.
- The mender of the Great Schism is now scripted to never convert in AI hands.
- Custom crusader state duchies are no longer legitimizable; custom titles are to be avoided due to messing up e.g. completely_controls assumptions related to titles.
- The Teutonic Order now only spawns if the HRE is held by a Catholic/Fraticelli non-heretic non-excommunicated ruler without an Anti-pope, seeing as their spawn event claims they get joint Papal-HRE sponsorship.
- Fixed portraits in Chinese mourning events.
- Fixed Shogunate bloodlines not getting associated with the correct religion.
- Mass Conversions can no longer result in you getting the option to swap to Gavelkind if you can, in fact, not swap to Gavelkind.
- If women for whatever reason can join your holy orders when it is relevant the Pope can now request that a female relative joins a holy order before crowning you, as opposed to only being able to target men. Does not normally come into play, but...
- The Grand Master of the Assassinate can now get missions.
- MO members no longer give themselves missions.
- SRS members no longer give themselves missions.
- DW members no longer give themselves missions.
- If the person giving you a mission in the Hermetics is not your apprentice they will now be more likely to have a higher society rank.
- Barons now pass on Japanese government traits on_death.
- The new Shogun's unlanded children and grandchildren (of the relevant gender(s)) now go Japanese Feudal alongside them.
- The guy with the funny hat no longer gets to tell the ERE/etc. to swap to Gavelkind from their elective law if they for whatever reason have elected not to disabuse him of the delusion that they listen to him.
- There's no longer a "+0" opinion towards food gatherers returning to court.
- You no longer get informed about where Fredak's lair is multiple times in a row.
- You should now be able to tell people other than your liege that you're not interested in brewing potions for them with your own ingredients in exchange for literally nothing.
- Added a notification event for when your close relative, vassal, or liege starts to mourn.
- Practice duels no longer give you the option to go "I won't stand this mockery!" right after telling the other person "I think you're a good friend", because that's silly.
- GHW announcements should now show the defender rather than potentially showing a third party title holder not at all involved in the GHW.
- The guests at your Hanami will no longer disparage your efforts to personally improve your garden if you're sufficiently good at it.
- You should no longer get a "My LNY/Chuseok has started!" event if it has in fact been cancelled.
- The Hanami should no longer fail to continue past the planning stage and lock you into being busy forever if none of your vassals decide to show up.
- Some stuff concerning dragons might now turn out to be relevant to divination/omens.
- "Eat rats?" made less spammy.
- Prestige loss from eating rats is now somewhat affected by real_tier.
- If you are/have recently engaged in ancestor worship and convert to a religion that's not in favour of that you'll no longer get events about continuing with ancestor worship or your ancestors helping/not helping.
- Patron deities problematic for your gender Doctrine are now cleaned up on conversion.
- It is no longer possible to pick a patron deity that's incompatible with your gender Doctrine.
- Inaccessible characters no longer get SoA relic events.
- Inaccessible characters no longer get to visit SoA monasteries.
- The Demon Spawn should no longer ask to become a monk/nun.
- Characters with a secret religion should not ask to become a monk/nun.
- Opened up SoA "Child asks to join monastery" event to non-Christians with monks/nuns. The event unfortunately has no AI logic, but...
- Councillors that end up incapable or inaccessible (e.g. in seclusion) can no longer continue artefact hunts.
- Sacrificing a courtier or a prisoner to the ancestors now costs piety for a pacifist. For some reason, vanilla did not think it should...
- Your relatives no longer get to completely circumvent gender Doctrines when asking you to press their claims.
- Wars declared to press your relatives' claims when they ask in an event are now declared on the top liege rather than the target if the target is a vassal.
- Fixed some treatment edge cases.
- Fixed some edge cases with Sickly child treatments.
- Vassals' provinces no longer convert during a Mass Conversion if said provinces belong to an unrelated religion.
- SR communities created during a faked Mass Conversion no longer stick around indefinitely, instead sticking around the 50 years they stick around in other circumstances.
- Fake Mass Converting rulers no longer get SR communities in provinces that did not have their religion prior to the fake conversion.
- Mass Converting vassals of vassals, as well as courtiers, whose true religion is the religion the realm is converting to will no longer go "I will fight and die for my fake religion!" and fake their conversion.
- Bön pagans that falsely convert during a Mass Conversion now gain the expected secret communities.
- Forsaking the faith of your ancestors, or helping others forsake the faith of theirs, can now upgrade nicknames.
- Adjustments to Mass Conversion nickname logic.
- You now only get a nickname for sponsoring a Mass Conversion if the target is a king+, to keep everyone from gaining "super cool" nicknames.
- Secret cannibals can now be canonized without causing a scandal.
- Secret cannibals will no longer be exposed as cannibals if scandalously canonized for another reason.
- If you are the Spymaster and escape an assassination attempt on yourself when investigating plots for your liege you'll now discover the plot regardless of whether your liege cares.
- You now need sufficient relative Intrigue to have a chance of successfully assassinating a Spymaster investigating your plots.
- You now need sufficient relative Intrigue to have a chance of escaping assassination attempts when investigating plots.
- DWs now actually sacrifice their targets in a fashion that'll show up in their kill lists.
- copy_random_personality_trait replaced with partial_random_personality_trait_copy_prev_effect in a couple of places.
- More fixing of Japanese government inheritance.
- Guardians no longer get to attempt to secretly convert ascetic children.
- Children under the age of 6 will no longer read or write letters while mourning.
- Increased wealth needed by liege for a PR to be able to transfer money, but also increased minimum amount taken.
- Added comments to event options when a physician chooses a treatment; makes the file more readable.
- Prevented Japanese government switching for people that'd immediately get a "Wrong holding!" issue and possibly a second, unwanted, government swap as a result.
- Added sanity check to ensure that Lowborn children conceived as bastards indeed are considered bastards.
- Fixed new pretender emperors forced to rename their title sometimes temporarily losing Chinese Imperial.
- Event 1047 once again correctly removes Gregarious rather than Gluttonous.
- It is no longer to offer a member of the Imperial Family (or the Ryukyuan knockoff's family) to China as a commander or physician if you should care, seeing as it'd disinherit them; a similar restriction already existed for concubines and eunuchs.
- Fixed broken check related to the EoC owning the artefact type you're trying to offer him.
- The EoC will no longer consider granting an artefact tribute to someone that has the same artefact already.
- Fixed "Ask Liege for Land [for Son/Daughter]" event.
- Fixed the AI not checking that its would-be consort actually is male when using Ainu WL powers as a female character.
- The Lotus Guard should no longer end up held by someone with an incorrect religion.
- Mudzaffar Shah now renames himself even if unlanded.
- WotRS members should now have a better idea of whether their religion or someone else's religion is "too foreign".
- WotRS members should now be fairly sure about their own gender, the gender of others, and whether they or the society take issue with either.
- Fixed a few cases where characters flagged to never convert would.
- If a raiding adventurer converts after settling their courtiers will not follow suit if they believe in an unrelated religion.
- Zealous AI Catholic rulers no longer convert when the Great Schism is mended.
- Fixed various instances of noreligion conversion.
- Fixed some leftover instances of the Saio/Saiin trying to become Japanese Monastic Feudal.
- Vassals and below in China now gain 25 % extra Grace for each vassal influence law in their favour.
- Grace gain for the EoC's GC is now increased by 50 %  for each GC influence law in their favour, stacking with the above.
- Title inheritance from the EoC or someone he cares about no longer causes you to suffer a Grace penalty.
- The Tenno is now always going to demand to be the senior party in any marriage, even within the Imperial Family.
- Landed AI members of the Imperial Family can now be selected to convert to Shinto if the Imperial Family is running low on possible heirs.
- Added some sanity checks to Aclla flavour events.
- Added weight_multipliers to many random Tianxia events.
- Improved portrait setup for Sunrise/Sunset invasion events.
- Your guards now do as you command when you tell them to imprison the person attempting to convert you in seclusion.
- Fixed incorrect scope when relatives are not-so-stealthy when trying to leave seclusion.
- Improved portrait setup in seclusion events.
- Duan Siping and Xu Zhigao are now more faction-happy in the IC start, and the latter brought friends. May you live in interesting times if you play as Da Yining or Wu.
- Events making use of the assassination button in script can no longer target the Tenno or the knockoff if you should care, consistent with the assassination button.
- Greatly reduced the time between the second-to-last and last events in the Hermetic and RAS laboratory infiltration mission event chains when successful.
- Fixed several show_tooltip_change = no issues that made it look as if you'd change your opinion of yourself.
- Increased the opinion threshold for the AI to NOT ask for additional powers in CI/EI realms.
- Nomadic provinces should no longer be prospering or be depopulated, with a cleanup on_yearly_pulse.
- Added a way for the AI to faction for valid claimants for the faction even if they are not valid candidates in an election, checked on_yearly_pulse or on_bi_yearly_pulse depending on their tier and whether their liege is independent or not. The player has a title_decision instead, but apparently the AI goes insane and attempts to support non-existent claimants if it has access to the decision.
- Eternal Life mystics are now quicker to introduce themselves once they show up.
- Loosened requirements on Children of Destiny to let them spawn with only AI parents if supernatural events are Unrestricted.
- Minor adjustments to RIP namespace event portrait setup.
- Made the AI a lot more eager to mourn, though also loosened the restrictions on refusal if their liege isn't someone that'd care.
- It should now be a bit clearer who'd be drinking the humiliating potion/poison when someone asks you to provide one.
- Various DW societies should no longer confuse their Demon Spawn with some other Demon Spawn. The latter is not the Dark Messiah, she/he's just a naughty girl/boy!
- Fixed some instances of Court Physicians ignoring Sickly children.
- Loosened a few checks on Court Physician treatment attempts to cover a few possible edge cases.
- The AI can now experience the End of Days.
- Lowered frequency of AI claimant faction workaround checks against vassal dukes and by counts.
- Added proper inaccessibility checks to A Focus on Family + Self-Improvement synergy relative selection.
- Improved portrait setup in the Tianxia namespace.

Landed titles:
- Added a new company of ship mercs in Japan.
- e_india now takes dynasty names.
- Loosened the creation conditions for e_india (if the decision already has been taken).

CBs:
- It is no longer possible to declare a tributary war (either kind) on a theocratic (is_theocracy = yes) religion head with the same religion regardless of whether you have an Antipope; fixes silly things like a HRE with an Antipope making the Pope a tributary and then forcing him to defend in Antipope wars.
- Liberation revolt leaders will now only become known as "the Liberator" if they have enough counties to (in theory) create or usurp the kingdom; liberating a small amount of counties is unimpressive.
- The AI will now execute secondary rebel uprising leaders on war victory.
- Rebel uprising leaders now get executed in a more suitable fashion.
- Wars of Honor no longer have ticking warscore for the defender being unoccupied.
- Wars of Honor now have a 2.0 battle warscore multiplier.
- It is no longer possible to declare a War of Honor on someone that has a regent; that'd be pretty dishonorable.
- Former CI rulers that lose their empires through some special CBs should now have their Mandate modifiers cleared out properly.
- Sanity fixes regarding holy war distance-type checks; if the attacker(s vassals and below) doesn't own any provinces in the de jure, they now need to be within one/two sea zones/adjacent to a province owned by the defender in the de jure, as opposed to *any* province within the de jure. Should result in fewer crimes against bordergore.
- Selfish winners of the First Crusade now also get a bloodline if HF is active. It might come with downsides, however...
- Fixed Shogunate bloodlines not getting associated with the correct religion.
- Mandate revolters now need a touch more impressive realms to claim the Mandate.
- The new Shogun's unlanded children and grandchildren (of the relevant gender(s)) now go Japanese Feudal alongside them when winning a Shogunate War.
- Fixed issue with the Gosannen War CBs (i.e. the "Northern Disturbance" wars).
- Fang La's rebellion can now invalidate if there's no land to seize in Liangzhe.
- crusade and new_crusade now have hostile_against_others = yes; religious zealots don't tend to be all that discerning when it comes to different brands of heresy/heathenry/apostasy/etc., and if there are co-religionists that are waging their own war for the title then they really should have joined the rel head's war.
- The Empire of a Thousand Ships (i.e. the TF-related pretender empire) will now set the de jure capital of the invaded kingdom as its preferred capital.

Decisions:
- Vassal kings can now set a Crown Focus at the same holding limit as vassal dukes; why would a king be worse in this regard, vanilla?
- Fixed icon for many religion conversion targeted decisions.
- Special religion swap self-targeted decisions (e.g. Shinto -> Shugendo) are now also available if you have a friend, lover, or non-rival close relative with the relevant religion; increases player freedom.
- Added an icon to potion-related targeted decisions for Hermetics and RAS members.
- Non-nomadic Raiding Adventurers should no longer be recruiting Horse Archers.
- Enlisted Ronin are now a lot cheaper to maintain.
- Prevented Heritage/Faith focus swap requests if the child is under the age of 6, seeeing as you otherwise can do some very exploitable things.
- Restricted Heritage/Faith focus swap requests a bit more in Chinese-/Japanese-style realms.
- Added icon related to Zun judging your prisoners (and hopefully finding them wanting).
- The player can now secretly convert to a lover's religion using the same targeted decision as for friends/spouses/consorts.
- Some more decisions concerning the Imperial Family now use the Imperial Family's mon.
- Taoist divination now has an icon.
- Removed some redundant independence checks.
- Correctly blocked ranking up to Hatamoto rank if there already is seven of those.
- Made the AI more interested in increasing/decreasing Regency Power.
- Made it possible to decrease Regency Power if your Permanent Regent currently isn't your actual regent but still is your vassal (e.g. due to them having a regent due being a child).
- The player should now consistently be blocked from ranking up in societies if inaccessible/incapable/imprisoned, excepting automatic "You became the grand master!" rank ups.
- You may no longer become the Saoshyant if you have a secret religion.
- You may no longer Mend the Great Schism if you have a secret religion.
- Fixed the Muslim Lord/Lady of India bloodline not getting associated with the correct religion.
- Telling your concubine to take monastic vows now breaks the concubine relationship even if she's the junior party.
- Moved AI Hwarang Cash Out to an on_five_year_pulse event.
- Decisions swapping your Japanese government now have your unlanded children and grandchildren (of the relevant gender(s)) swap alongside you.
- Various employment decisions now have sane caps on their scaled_wealth.
- Added a decision for king+ rulers capable of appointing a Court Alchemist to recruit one if they lack suitable non-rival candidates.
- Several title founding decisions can now upgrade nicknames if the nickname they'd hand out are cooler than your current nickname.
- Forming the Slavic Union or the Russian Empire now also requires controlling the original de jures of the relevant kingdoms, not just the kingdoms, seeing as the kingdoms could have become titular.
- You can no longer ask to swap a child's focus to Heritage/Faith if you, the child, or the child's host is inaccessible, incapable, or imprisoned.
- Added a new decision for the Aztecs and the Inca to summon reinforcements from back home, because why wouldn't they if they felt the need?
- Studying the Art of War is now less likely to be stressful for Strategists and those with a Martial education.
- The EoC no longer loses Grace for making a princess available for regular marriage, seeing as Grace is meaningless for him and his heir.
- Added a copy of "Introduce Heir to the Realm" to the Intrigue menu, seeing as I keep forgetting to take the decision on my heir as it doesn't show up as a high priority alert.
- Members of the Ainu WL can now ask their lovers to become their consorts even if the lovers in question have other lovers. Don't expect them to break up with those other lovers, however...
- Rel heads can no longer be intimidated into/out of factions with Nivkh WL powers under any circumstances.
- Much like PRs, Grand Chancellors can no longer be intimidated into factions with Nivkh WL powers.
- Made the AI wait a bit longer between checking if it wants to impose a quarantine.
- Lowered cooldown on Sohei/Shaolin training to five years (was ten).
- Nomads may no longer hold Summer Fairs.
- Nomadic provinces should no longer prosper from decisions.
- Fixed the Honor for being eligible to call more influential WotRS members into wars being wrong.
- Told the AI to consider giving a Mark of Dishonor more frequently when it is warranted.
- Adjusted the AI's willingness to prepare Treasure Fleets a bit.
- Adjusted the AI's willingness to ask to join the Treasure Fleet a bit.
- Made the AI consider raising Imperial Guard regiments a bit less often.
- The AI is no longer far more fond of retaking control of the the Tianwu Guard than it is to retake control of the others.
- Vassals can now raise Warrior Bands using the Gosannen War-related bloodlines' special decision, but they need to spend prestige to do so.
- The Gosannen War-related bloodlines' Warrior Bands can now only be raised as a primary participant in a war.
- Warrior Bands now have a lower maintenance cost.
- The AI now considers raising Warrior Bands a bit more frequently.
- The Stoics, Bacchants, and Disciples of Mount Kailash can no longer be founded from inside a prison cell.
- It is now free to revoke your title's capital county if you have no counties (only affects the AI).
- The AI now checks whether it can create a Jurchen pretender empire with the special decision twice as often (every 60 months).
- You can no longer ask to swap an ascetic child to Faith/Heritage.
- Pretender emperors now get a discount on special JD characters, though not as much as the EoC gets.
- Fixed the decisions to ask for fewer restrictions/more powers as a GC/vassal having the wrong prestige requirement, which could result in it leaving you at negative prestige.
- Increased wealth needed by liege for a PR to be able to transfer money, but also increased minimum amount taken.
- Starting a dragon hunt now immediately flags you with do_not_disturb.
- Prevented Japanese government switching for people that'd immediately get a "Wrong holding!" issue and possibly a second, unwanted, government swap as a result.
- Prevented decision-based founding of Heian-kyo, Cairo, and Habsburg while at war; moving your capital and stuff like that could result in weird stuff.
- Made the AI more willing to interview fellow RAS members.
- You can now only ask your liege to experiment on his prisoners if you are his Court Alchemist.
- The AI now only cares about organizing Hwarang patrols in its own demesne.
- The AI now only cares about combating disease with the RAS power in its own demesne.
- Xu Zhigao should no longer go "My name is Li Bian, my realm is Tang... but I am *not* the true Son of Heaven!".
- You now need sufficient prestige (and Grace, if not the EoC) to remain at or above zero when making a princess available for regular marriage.
- It is no longer to offer a member of the Imperial Family (or the Ryukyuan knockoff's family) to China as a commander or physician if you should care, seeing as it'd disinherit them; a similar restriction already existed for concubines and eunuchs.
- Fixed broken check related to the EoC owning the artefact type you're trying to offer him.
- Fixed "Ask Liege for Land [for Son/Daughter]" decision.
- Only actual followers of the religion of an SRS that goes public now get to convert, not everyone sympathetic towards the converter's religion group; helps fix some of the nonsense surrounding SRSes.
- Vushmgir adopting his brother's religion now has an effect on MA.
- Yngvar/Igor abandoning his religion now has an effect on MA.
- Yngvar/Igor can no longer adopt "local traditions" if the local traditions are not the expected local traditions.
- Baron-tier Court Alchemists can now also request ingredients.
- It is now possible to extend the ongoing quarantine in a province after six months have passed, as opposed to having to wait until the quarantine has ended. Should make it less likely you forget to renew it right as it expires, and also helps the AI.
- Increased the opinion threshold for the AI to NOT ask for additional powers in CI/EI realsm.
- Added a player-only title decision to get around the silly "If you can't be elected your claims can't be pressed in a faction" thing (where sensible; there are still restrictions on e.g. factioning against the Tenno if you should care or if the claimant is a random person that should care).
- Hopefully eradicated all remaining cases of nomads settling with dynamic titles when there's no reason for them to use one of those, seeing as those can cause issues.
- General AI logic tweaks.
- All Intrigue menu decisions should now have an icon.

Objectives:
- Vassal bureaucrats can now attempt to See the Realm Prosper at any realm size, as intended.
- Vassal kings and dukes large enough to set a Crown Focus can now attempt to See the Realm Prosper.
- Vassal MRs can now attempt to See the Realm Prosper at any size.
- Sub-vassals of nomads can no longer attempt to See the Realm Prosper, seeing as their nomadic liege likely wishes to burninate their stuff with prejudice.
- Claimant factions will no longer form for duchies unless the holder is a duke.
- Claimant factions will never form for counties.
- It is once again possible to select "Groom an Heir" while inaccessible.
- It is no longer possible to be in or lead more than one succession law faction of the same type at the same time, seeing as there's no way for the interface to tell you which ultimatum you'd be issuing.
- Becoming a Famous Writer now only requires a combined artefact quality of 7 or more (down from 10 or more).
- Fabrication plots are now properly only permitted against de jure liege titles and above.
- Trying to become a Paragon of Virtue should no longer sometimes auto-fail pretty much right away.
- It is no longer possible to pick or keep the ambitions to gain land for a son/daughter or yourself from a liege with a Permanent Regency, seeing as it is impossible to ask them for land.
- Characters in seclusion or in hiding can no longer be in (or lead) factions.
- If you are in seclusion your council members now get to join factions as if the council was discontent, seeing as they do not get to bask in your august presence.
- Pagans eligible for Chinese-/Japanese-style governments now seek to become Paragons of Enlightenment rather than Paragons of Virture. Should perhaps be extended to more pagans in the future.
- It is no longer possible for ascetic children to have the Faith or Heritage focus or for it to be selected for them; they're not going to convert willingly. Generally affects historical characters.
- Incapable characters, as well as characters with a secret religion and DW society members, no longer get to keep trying to strengthen their religion.
- Incapable characters now lose the "Reject [bad trait]" ambitions if they have them.
- Children for "Gain land for an unlanded Son/Daughter" now need to be in your court.
- The Acllahuasi is no longer interested in independence from the Sapa Inca.
- Fixed the Takeover faction incorrectly not being available against non-CI rulers.
- Told the AI how religion works in China/Japan/etc. when it comes to claimant factions (i.e. a close enough religion is not grounds for immediate dismissal of a claimant).
- Told the AI that if they are the old female claimant it's not a problem that the female claimant is old.
- Told the AI that immortal women under the practical_age of 40 are sane candidates even if they've been alive for over 40 years.
- Told the AI that various elective (and "elective") laws other than Feudal/Princely Elective generally means it might not *stay* the heir and that "But I'm the heir!" thus isn't grounds to immediately disband its own faction.
- Told the AI that if it isn't a Muslim then "The candidate is a Sayyid/Mirza!" is heathen nonsense that's not worth considering when considering how good a claimant is.
- Told the AI that "I'm in the same society as the claimant!", while certainly an argument in favour of that claimant over another, is not really a good reason to support the claimant if the current title holder also is in that same society.
- Saoshyant, Saoshyant Descendant, and Amaterasu Descendant now give similar boosts to claimants as Sayyid/Mirza when it comes to factioning.
- Confucius' male line descendants, male line descendants of Jimmu, and those that are part of a Divine Ancestry bloodline now give similar boosts to claimants as Sayyid/Mirza when it comes to factioning.

Graphics:
- Iterated a bit on the new society quest backgrounds, primarily the seals.
- All gender laws now have appropriate symbols, as opposed to vanilla's less appropriate setup.
- Fixed the WotRS commander trait using the wrong icon.

History:
- Rollo now converts on a more appropriate date and also gets baptized as Rollo, and also changes his culture appropriately.
- William Longsword is now born when he ought to be, and converts alongside his father.
- Matilda di Canossa now founds her historical bloodline 1077.1.28.
- Matilda di Canossa and Gottfried the Hunchback now start betrothed, seeing as they were. The player can of course elect to break the betrothal ahead of the marriage.
- Charlie now dies when he should.
- The First Crusade is now considered to be over when it actually ended, preventing you from gaining the relevant bloodline if you start in the small gap between when the Kingdom of Jerusalem is created and when the vanilla devs decided to add the flag (which also didn't line up with when they had set the Jihads to start because Jerusalem "had" fallen; very inconsistent reasoning...).
- Fang La and Lü Shinang are now perceived as rebel scum as far as Huizong of Song is concerned.
- There's now a global "tianxia" flag set early on; has no effect for us, but of use potentially for others...
- Bjørn Farmann now has his nickname.
- Erik Bloodaxe's kill list should now be more accurate. He knows what he did.
- Rajaraja the Great now dies closer to when he should.
- Fixed mistake that made William Longsword go by the name of Rollo.
- Arnulf the Great is now credited for his murder of William Longsword.
- William Longsword now starts as an Inspiring Leader, Wroth, Brave, and with slightly more base Martial, seeing as he was a fairly decent commander but maybe a bit rash.
- William Longsword now gets excommunicated roughly on schedule in the history files (between playable dates).
- Sverre of Norway is now excommunicated from 1194.6.15, seeing as he should be.
- John of England is now excommunicated 1209.11.1-1213.5.15, seeing as he should be.
- Sima Guang now gets his bloodline in 1085, seeing as he didn't become Grand Chancellor before then.
- Fixed some killers and missing kinslayer traits for Later Tang's rulers and relatives. They know what they did.
- Fixed a mess involving some Catalans (and one Occitan).

Localisation:
- Fixed missing word in Matilda di Canossa's bloodline and its generic versions.

- The Great Blot loc should no longer assume the Allfather/Odin, Thor, and Frey are your deities of choice; they might be perceived as evil if you went Enatic Clans, for one thing...

- Tianxia's WLs no longer claim you require a Norse neighbour if you're not of the appropriate religion.
- Bloodlines related to overseas raiding no longer speak of cities being "decimated"; who cares about one tenth of the population?
- A bunch of events that previously referred to a character's location now refer to their capital, reducing the likelihood of stuff like "My dear vassal, I'll be hosting a feast in the Pacific Ocean soon!".
- Fixed "-Centralization" in varions Chinese interest group laws.
- Fixed assumption in Ancestor Worship loc regarding a female "elder" being involved.
- Fixed DW mission events sometimes overflowing.
- Non-religious artefacts that are looted will no longer involve a reference to religious significance.
- The Assassins should no longer get very confused about their religion and stuff related to it if a member isn't openly Shia.
- Fixed some minor CB loc oddities.
- Fixed "Japanese enough" characters sometimes being far more concerned about non-co-religionist Japanese holy orders than people with entirely different religions.
- Warrior Lodge members now reference the correct "priests" for the society in question.
- MOs are no longer assumed to be an "Order".
- Plots involving tragic falls no longer assume the child lives in a castle; vanilla apparently never updated that for The Republic or anything else...
- War focus war games should no longer have you speak of the "center flank".
- If your liege comes along and asks for a Heritage/Faith swap you'll no longer always think they're trying to turn the ward into a savage.
- power_immortality_potion_power_desc now has proper English localisation, as opposed to having English localisation set as French.
- Fixed a couple of broken strings with "INTERACTION_INTERACTION" rather than "INTERACTION".
- Fixed some very minor MoH update loc issues.
- WotRS mentor/student events now make better use of event targets in the loc.

- Assorted minor loc tweaks.

Execution methods:
- Zoroastrians will no longer burn people to death (outside events).
- Traitors in Chinese-style realms can now be executed in a historically appropriate fashion (no; not nine relations).

- Added a new execution method for Chinese-style realms.

Traits:
- The Cannibal trait is now usually hidden from the world at large.
- Added new exposed_cannibal trait to deal with the cases where someone has been rather overt about eating at the red table; currently in a separate file to ensure saves aren't broken.
- Removed +15 attraction opinion from Tribal Kinslayer; why would random people find that attractive? It's not limited to tribals or anything...
- Added +5 attraction opinion to the Poet trait; I hear romantic poetry is a thing...
- Added +5 PCS (Go for the eyes!) to the Falconer trait, seeing as the change to Poet made it comparatively worse... and this also makes the traits have a different effect when you get the "Falconer or Poet?" event chain.

Job actions:
- "Settle Tribe" now actually checks if culture can spread navally if culture spread is more restricted, as opposed to assuming that it always can spread.

Scripted effects:
- Silk Road religion conversion should now inform the correct characters.

- Fixed Wako raiders sometimes having multiple lifestyles.
- RAS members that have brewed a successful immortality potion are now a bit better at brewing immortality potions (or "immortality potions") with the desired effects if they set out to brew one later on.
- Society members that are dying should not also inform certain people that they have "left the society".
- Characters should no longer think highly of others for guarding their food after leaving seclusion.
- Food gathering expeditions are now slightly better at their job.
- The "the Unchaste" nickname is now only handed out when adultery is discovered, as opposed to quite literally being something a Chaste Celibate character that's never unfaithful could get sometimes. Vanilla logic sure is something...
- Tweaked weights for the "the Decadent" nickname.
- "the Clueless" now requires being more clueless.
- "the Beguiling" is no longer for unattractive people.
-  The "the Shrewd" and the "the Wily" nicknames are no longer for dumb people, and are a bit more likely for smarter people.
- Shrewd people should no longer become known as "the Thin-Minded" or "the Forgetful", and Dull people have joined other dumb people in being more likely to get those nicknames.
- The MoH now cares more about relative realm size for other bureaucracies in general.
- Added new partial_random_personality_trait_copy_prev_effect.
- The EoC's Grace is now zeroed on every yearly Mandate update; he shouldn't need it, and if he's deposed he'll certainly not be in China's good graces.
- Pretender emperors that somehow have positive Grace now lose it on their next yearly Mandate update.
- Pretender empire titles will now get a preferred capital set when they are created, ensuring there is a capital to revoke by decision and nudging the AI towards a suitable capital. The holder's capital's kingdom's de jure capital will be used.
- Removed unused set_best_mourning_target_effect.

Opinion modifiers:
- Giving someone a helpful potion now increases opinion, as opposed to decreasing it.

- Japonic, Vietic, and Koreanic characters will no longer name their children after their parents or grandparents.

Scripted triggers:
- Excepting the Tenno, you are now permitted to rename yourself at any age. Unfortunately, this runs into some weirdness with the Wikipedia button; sorcery or spaghetti code?
- Seeing as they can't use the decision anyway, "Chinese enough" and "Japanese enough" pagans will not get an alert related to Mass Conversions.

On actions:

Governments:

Dynasties:

Crusades:

This should also fix a tooltip, which of course is nice.
