Beta 14.1.0 - 28/06/2024 - Potions, Poisons, and Peaches
--------------------------------------

IMPORTANT: Tianxia has been rebased off of CleanSlate; CleanSlate 1.0 Beta 6.1 [EZKM] (https://github.com/ck2plus/CleanSlate/releases/tag/1.0-beta6.1) MUST be active for Tianxia to run.
IMPORTANT: Any old Tianxia.mod file MUST be replaced with the new one; the new version contains important changes, such as actually setting up CleanSlate as a dependency.
IMPORTANT: Delete any old Tianxia directory; don't just overwrite it with the new one.
IMPORTANT: Pre-14.1.0 saves will not work due to traits being added.
IMPORTANT: Random/Shattered world and the EU4 Converter are not supported.
IMPORTANT: Start dates after 1127.4.20 remain unsupported; it's possible to start in them, but they're not expected to be in a good spot, and are not a priority to fix.

CBs:
- Fixed a bug with Japan not being usurped by the new Shogun.
- The (Roman) Imperial Reconquest CB now checks for the "Trajan's Borders" geographical region, which approximately corresponds to Trajan's empire and which cares not for silly things like de jure drift.
- The (Roman) Imperial Reconquest CB check regarding the defender's religion now makes sense.
- Claimants installed through a war now lose all relevant ascetic traits/modifiers.
- The Imperial Claim CB (the regular version, not the faction version) is now only available for 1) close relatives of the current Tenno that are NOT descendants of the current Tenno, 2) close relatives of previous holders of the Chrysanthemum Throne and previous holders of the Chrysanthemum Throne, and 3) rivals of the current Tenno, which should make some members of the Imperial Family less likely to go "I don't want an NAP with the Tenno due to having a CB!".
- Wars of Honor can no longer be declared if inaccessible, incapable, or imprisoned.
- Wars of Honor can no longer be declared against someone that is inaccessible, incapable, or imprisoned. That would be most dishonourable.
- Rising Sun Subjugations can no longer be declared if inaccessible, incapable, or imprisoned.
- CI rulers are now correctly blocked from pressing third party non-de jure claims. Should perhaps be extended to bureaucrats in general, seeing as the Tenno and the knockoff already are blocked...
- The Tenno's claims (and those of the knockoff) can no longer be pressed by other parties. That might make those people get ideas about not being mere figureheads, and we can't have that!
- William the Conqueror now imposes the Plantagenet CoA alongside the Norman Yoke; it's still early, but it's a better date than pre-Roman times.
- If William somehow takes over England, loses it, and then takes it back with another invasion he no longer gets to usurp duchies and impose Primogeniture "Because William!".
- Told the AI that it shouldn't holy war land held by nomads just because someone else than the nomad owns a de jure county that contains a suitable barony.
- Told the AI not to liberate nomad duchies just because someone else holds a suitable barony in the de jure.
- It is no longer permissible to declare a GHW of any kind if you've got a non-co-religionist rel head liege or above, seeing as that's quite silly and you in theory could be going after someone said liege or above approves of or otherwise could be up to truly silly things (e.g. a Shia Caliph declaring such a war while under the Sunni Caliph).
- Tribal and Nomadic Invasions, as well as Nomadic Subjugations, can now only be declared against neighbours, excepting special event exceptions (i.e. the starting wars involving the Mongols, Aztecs, and Inca).
- The AI should now respect its primary title's gender law a bit more when picking a Crusade beneficiary.
- The Fourth Crusade should now only use custom kingdoms as the last resort, seeing as they create assorted scripting problems.
- Christian crusades that end in victory will now use the gender law of the primary participant for the crusader kingdom.
- The Children's Crusade no longer gets to usurp Jerusalem from unrelated parties.
- The Children's Crusade will now create/usurp d_jerusalem as well as k_jerusalem if possible and sensible.
- The Children's Crusade now gets a more proper succession and gender law setup.
- Seeing as you presumably include a clause to the effect of "The gender law can be ignored!" when fabricating a claim, a personally held strong claim may once more be pressed by women on Agnatic titles and men on Enatic titles when using the Claim CB; consistent with vanilla, if not with CleanSlate. Notably, the Claim All CB doesn't check gender laws at all...
- The various brands of the Subjugation CB should no longer be visible vs. your liege(s).
- Nickname handouts now respect "coolness" and also allow for upgrading.
- "the Dragoness" is now used where appropriate.
- Only actual Vikings (including upgraded ones that'd have had the base Viking trait earlier) need apply for the "the Viking" nickname.
- "Hardrada" is now only for North Germanics.
- Penglai Expedition restrictions added.
- Manifesting your destiny is no longer compatible with having a Chinese or Japanese-style government.
- William the Conqueror should once again get his nickname if he wins the Norman Conquest.
- Tributary tier abdications no longer happen if the character being abdicated to belongs to another dynasty, as that might cause a game over.
- A failed TF invasion now correctly results in the disbanding of the relevant event forces.
- TF invasions now check and clear the correct province flags.
- Other Claim vs. liege CBs should now work as expected when a faction is involved.
- Fixed "¨" in other_claim_on_liege.
- Fixed an issue with the True Believers' war.
- AI crusade beneficiaries now also need to be capable and not imprisoned.

Societies:
- Taoists can no longer access the Hermetics.
- Added the Renowned Apothecary Society to various checks.
- Taoists can no longer sneak into the Hermetics before the game starts.
- Told various Koreans that "But the game just started!" isn't sufficient justification to join the Hwarang if the society in question isn't around.
- Looking like Odin is now considered a disadvantage in the Fellowship of Hel, rather than an advantage.

Events:
- Hermetics and Alchemists should no longer destroy inherited ingredients.
- Chinese leadership traits are now available for all Chinese culture group characters, not just Han characters, in the events that are dependent on culture.
- Fixed possible breaking of the Dragonslayer event chain.
- Fredak's lair should now be properly flagged and properly event targeted everywhere.
- Added joining event for the Alchemists.
- Set up AI joining for the Alchemists.
- The EoC should now remember to actually hand titles to vassals/courtiers when deciding to do so, as opposed to just telling them that they should expect the county "any day now".
- Dragons no longer force an eternal LNY.
- Added society power events for the Renowned Apothecary Society.
- Assorted minor Hermetics bugfixing.
- Added new Roman province events.
- Added Court Alchemist events.
- Barbarians no longer need apply for Roman province events.
- Claimants installed through a faction ultimatum now lose all relevant ascetic traits/modifiers.
- Fredak and Icava no longer care for non-feudals.
- The wonder selected when drinking from the Fountain of Youth now actually needs to possess the relevant upgrade, as opposed to being any random garden-type wonder in the realm.
- The age reduction from the Fountain of Youth is now a practical_age reduction rather than an actual age reduction.
- Immortals and Kitsune no longer randomly die in the name of performance.
- Immortal mystics now also found immortal bloodlines with HF if they become immortal.
- Added event chains for the brewing of immortality potions (including fake ones).
- The epic_hangover modifier is now handed out in a few more places.
- When the Mongols spawn Genghis and Börte's children now get the proper genetic father and mother. Jochi has a 50 % chance of being Temujin's.
- Hermetic and RAS lab builders now get better labs if they are non-Slothful Architects, particularly if Diligent.
- All "You got symptom X" events now have a 10 % chance of adding a "The symptoms got worse" modifier a bit down the line. This helps disguise where symptoms get worse due to side-effects from potions or poisons.
- Added events related to side-effects.
- The "End of Days" prophet can no longer be an existing courtier, seeing as giving one of those +3 health and a total of +22 to stats just because an event fires is a very silly vanilla implementation.
- Added a better gender setup for the "End of Days" prophet.
- Deprecated event RIP.15103, merging the religious tensions into event RIP.15102 and jumpting directly to RIP.15104; it changes basically nothing and avoids involving characters that are poorly selected by vanilla (e.g. imprisoned rivals or foreign rulers at war with you...).
- The EoD prophet's troops now have a set composition, as opposed to matching ROOT and thus potentially spawning War Elephants or the like.
- EoD peasant leaders have been taught to respect gender doctrines.
- EoD events now have quite a bit more AI logic, as opposed to vanilla's "Adding anything at all sounds like too much work!" approach...
- Rivals blamed as part of the EoD events now have some saner requirements.
- The eclipse EoD event can now affect your traits based on what you choose, and some options have further restrictions.
- You now need to be sufficiently more diplomatic than your EoD rival, or sufficiently better at intrigue, to sic a peasant mob on them.
- The EoD prophet now declares a claim war for your capital duchy rather than your primary title; fixes certain edge cases such as religious titles, and also seems more reasonably considering they spawn with at most 3k troops.
- Peasant leaders recruited to fight the living dead no longer get their liege blamed for killing children that they themselves kill.
- Hopefully fixed yet another Absolute Cognatic edge case in Japan.
- Ramirez the Immortal is now culturally Coptic. He's still a Scotsman as far as graphical_culture goes, of course.
- "Chinese enough" and Japonic characters no longer necessarily associate dragons with burning people to death.
- Adjustments to Chinese implosions, with the goal of creating somewhat saner and stronger breakaways; indirect vassals no longer get to pick a side/break free unless they are a rel head, and the general "Should I break away or even try becoming a pretender?" logic is a bit different. The result might still be very fractured if there are few multi-duke/king vassals.
- Symptoms that get worse (whether naturally or due to potion side effects) now restarts and re-rolls the "X days to symptom cleanup" event (unless the symptoms are due to a serious disease, in which case they're not cleared out until the disease itself is revealed).
- "Cleopatra" is now a Coptic Hellenic rather than an Egyptian Hurufi.
- The game will no longer make a courtier Depressed just so that someone looking for eternal life will have a Depressed character around.
- Eternal Life advisors can no longer progress their quest if they or you are imprisoned, incapable, or inaccessible. The event chain should not break, but it will pause.
- Added a bunch of AI logic to the Eternal Life event chain; vanilla effectively skipped it.
- Rival councillors are more likely to fail to find an Eternal Life mystic, friends and lovers slightly more likely to find one.
- Rival councillors are more likely to find a not-so-honest Eternal Life mystic when they find one.
- Eternal Life mystics no longer get as massive stat boosts from being non-frauds.
- The Eternal Life events now decide on the mystic's skill category when you pick the advisor, not when the mystic arrives, which means swapping councillors around won't result in odd things.
- Eternal Life quests no longer progress if you or the mystic are imprisoned, incapable, or inaccessible; the event chain will not break, but it will pause.
- The Eternal Life events no longer change congenital traits; the event chain might be flagged as supernatural, and the immortality outcome might be explicitly so, but the events where that is meant to happen are not.
- Falling to your death during the Eternal Life event chain no longer results in you supposedly being torn apart by a peasant mob.
- If one of your companions betray you and eat your apple during the Eternal Life event chain, they become a real character, gain the Immortal trait (and an Immortal bloodline, if HF is active), and become your rival.
- Eternal Life mystics will no longer convert. They've heard all your arguments before, and they didn't work then.
- Attempting to sacrifice an antelope or strangling a snake now costs piety if you are a pacifist.
- Sacrificing grain/antelopes or killing snakes no longer improve Martial; the former has nothing to do with fighting, and neither involves commanding armies.
- Fighting snakes no longer make you a Trickster; however sneaky your battlefield opponent is, they are not sssssnakes.
- The reincarnation child spawned in the Eternal Life event chain is now more sensibly set up (was complete and utter vanilla nonsense), and the heir might elect to dismiss the whole mess.
- AI reformers will no longer convert to any other religion.
- MoH revolters no longer have random traits, which helps with some silliness.
- Rise of the Shia revolters will no longer convert religion in AI hands.
- The Eternal Life branch about the Philosopher's Stone/alchemy now gives an advantage from better Hermetic/RAS labs and some related things.
- Mystics will only (truthfully) be caught as frauds if the investigator has sufficient Intrigue.
- Mystics will only catch on to fraud investigations if they have sufficient Intrigue.
- Should the Eternal Life mystic leave due to considering you a failure you will now gain an imprisonment reason towards them. They know what they did.
- Added new events to allow the AI to meddle in vassal wars.
- Added new events to allow the AI to demand Gavelkind.
- Non-fraud Eternal Life mystics now age up quite a lot should the quest succeed.
- Severe side-effects related to symptoms that can take a long while to expire now also have the potential to take a long while to expire.
- If the Hellenic religion is reformed with Enatic Clans the Myrmidons will instead be known as the Amazons. Looks like Penthesilea gets the last laugh, Achilleus!
- A Hellenic Roman (or a Hellenic Italian/Greek, or a Hellenic Atlantean) that burns Rome now damages the Apostolic Palace and sets the stage for a new decision.
- The "Torture prisoner?" event is now disabled if RD is active; it's better if everyone uses the decision in that case, particularly as it puts the AI logic in one place.
- Added prestige rewards for all the new Roman provinces.
- Nomads and tribals no longer get hospital flavour events, seeing as they aren't allowed to build them and as those events make some assumptions.
- Non-Christian theocratic rel heads can now also get in on the hospital money demands.
- Added/improved AI logic for hospital events.
- Set up special Roman province rewards.
- The Fourth Crusade relative of the ERE now needs to be from inside the ERE; a "prince(ess)" from abroad makes things weird.
- The Fourth Crusade relative of the ERE now needs to be Christian; anything else is just nonsensical.
- Both Romeo and Juliet now need to be adults; vanilla really screwed up on that one...
- You can no longer realize you share many interests with your spouse if you are inaccessible, incapable, or imprisoned, consistent with if they are any of those things.
- The EoC will no longer give concubines to imprisoned, incapable, or inaccessible relatives.
- Friends gained through the Theology focus are now picked in a saner fashion.
- Muslim husbands will no longer discover wives becoming lovers with each other if they're inaccessible, incapable, or imprisoned; event chain still needs several further fixes, but that's for a later patch.
- Muslim husbands will no longer potentially get any other wives that were not involved in the above event chain killed.
- Reverse-converting Lord Spirituals can no longer override secret religions or ai_flag_refuse_conversion. Vanilla silliness.
- Your bored spouse should no longer befriend an inaccessible, incapable, or imprisoned courtier.
- Sway/Antagonize event chains should no longer get confused as to the target of an ongoing sub-chain if you change your target; it will instead stick with the original target for the sub-chain.
- Chinese bloodline characters are now more likely to be strategists or siege engineers if you are at war.
- Ancestor worship lovers now need to share ROOT's religion, seeing as they otherwise have no reason to be present at the same ceremonies. Needs further work in a later patch.
- The ancestors will no longer help imprisoned, incapable, or inaccessible characters in delayed events; the flavour doesn't make sense for such characters.
- Court Chaplains are now more likely to catch apostates/DWs if their Intrigue is higher, and less likely to catch apostates/DWs/innocent victims if their Intrigue is lower, seeing as sneakier people are less likely to arouse suspicion in the first place.
- Monastic Order leaders can now get society quests.
- Monastic Order members can no longer attempt to befriend (or tumble) dead people or people that are imprisoned, incapable, or inaccessible.
- General accessibility sanity checks for MOs.
- Assorted Muslim honorary title events now have more checks for accessibility.
- It is now much rarer for tribal provinces to get the nifty Prosperity modifiers.
- Burninated provinces (and Fredak's lair) no longer prosper.
- Prosperity events no longer cause province religious conversion for unreformed pagans, Cosmopolitan/Tolerant religions, Dharmics on Dharmic provinces, "Chinese enough" characters on "Chinese enough" provinces, or "Japanese enough" characters on "Japanese enough" provinces.
- Tribals no longer get to convert non-tribal land through prosperity events.
- Pacifists are now less likely to get Regimental Grounds.
- Fixed Chinese implosions not actually imploding China but still handing out claims. Enjoy the Mingsplosions!
- Centres of Worship can no longer be established in provinces that have a relevant Great Work.
- A "Great Library" (province modifier) can no longer be established in a province with a Great Library or the House of Wisdom.
- People with a secret religion will now ignore all suggestions to establish a Centre of Worship for their public religion.
- A Scholar Foundation can no longer be established in a province with a Grand University.
- A Merchant Harbour can no longer be established if the province has a Grand Harbour.
- MRs are more likely to get to establish Merchant Harbours.
- Trade post provinces are slightly more likely to get Merchant Harbours.
- Added new Silk Road Market Prosperity modifier option.
- People with a Chinese government can now get the special JD province modifiers as part of the prosperity events if JD is active.
- Master Engineers will no longer hand out the special JD province modifiers in provinces without a castle or a city.
- Capped scaled_wealth costs and rewards in prosperity events.
- Improved Prosperity event AI logic, because vanilla had very little.
- The old EoC now gets to abdicate to a dynastic successor state even if e.g. imprisoned, seeing as that's nicer, particularly for the player.
- The exclave game rules that respect de jure now treat e_china and the China region as de jure for all CI rulers.
- The exclave game rules that respect de jure now treat e_japan and the Greater Japan region as de jure for all JI and DI rulers.
- Prosperity growth no longer cares about you helping out in another war quite as much.
- Recently depopulated provinces prosper more slowly, instead of prospering more quickly.
- Extensively overhauled the seclusion events, with the primary goals of making them less repetitive and less predictable (e.g. "If child wants to sneak out then child has peasant lover" is no longer always true), as well as making the food mechanic saner and the AI logic more robust and, you know, logical.
- The player now gets informed about their liege going into seclusion, much like the AI.
- Expelling someone from seclusion now makes them stop being your friend or lover.
- Characters in seclusion now gather food from their capital if possible when heading into seclusion.
- Characters in seclusion might now opt to have someone gather extra food, at the possible risk of them catching the disease. You could always decide not to let them back in...
- Excluding children, incapable rulers, and unplayable rulers, characters in seclusion now get to pick an approach to how they want to live in seclusion, such as attempting to pray for salvation or partying like there is no tomorrow. These different options have an impact on the flavour you might see, though some events are shared.
- Characters in seclusion can get the opportunity to appoint someone to guard their food against theft. This guard is of course in an excellent position to be bribed, to give food to their friends and relatives, to eat more on their own, or to be attacked by people that want more food. Choose wisely.
- Characters in seclusion now lose food in a much saner fashion, as opposed to the "You are about to run out of food!" event chain firing somewhat randomly. The amount of food lost depends on both event factors and the kind of seclusion you are having.
- Praying for the plague to just go away is an option; it's not really going to work out (read: There's no supernatural "Deity steps in" event), but it's certainly an option.
- Being in seclusion sure might get less boring if you treat it like a party. I'm sure the starving peasant rabble can be safely ignored; what are they going to do, launch some kind of attack?
- Keeping busy during seclusion is perhaps a mixed bag, but for some that might beat relaxing.
- Trying to conserve food while in seclusion is perhaps not the worst idea.
- Growing your own food in seclusion might be wise, but you might get dirty when engaging in such a lowly activity.
- The people that are up to no good in your court might perhaps not like it if you decide to look for secrets while in seclusion, but it might pay off... or not!
- Just taking the time to relax during seclusion -- no parties, no work, no gardening; nothing -- might be good for you.
- Relatives asking to leave seclusion are no longer guaranteed to have peasant lovers, and the peasant lover outcome has been replaced with a similar "heading to a brothel" outcome, seeing as the character caught sneaking out might be too young to reasonably have had a lover when the seclusion started.
- People other than your steward might now have an interest in hidden tunnels, and if the vanilla-based sub-chain fires it no longer guarantees a compatible courtier or some other odd things from vanilla.
- You should now generally only be considered to have been exposed to the outside if there has been a disease present in the relevant province(s) when someone enters seclusion (or when an event implies some interaction with someone from such a province), not if e.g. a tunnel sneaker enters before the plague arrives.
- Being secluded up with people that don't like you might result in fairly literal backstabbing.
- Favours might be gained by both you and by more ambitious relatives or voters in seclusion.
- The servants might take issue with certain traits in seclusion and get murdery, particularly if food is running out.
- A Cannibal that's engaged in some butchering might find that others also are becoming Cannibals. It's probably fine. Probably.
- Characters that are less fond of you and the whole seclusion idea might now demand that you open the gates. Refusing might not be wise; after all, perhaps your heir would be more open to seeing things their way...
- Having the guards clear out corpse piles is no longer guaranteed to expose you to the plague, but it'll hurt your capital levy/garrison.
- Being Lustful no longer means you get a pass to hug ill courtiers.
- You may now hug your sick friends. It's not particularly smart, but nobody will stop you.
- You no longet get to break up lovers in seclusion; you can tell them to stop, but they're not forced to listen.
- Lovers discovered through the normal (i.e. vanilla) seclusion lover discovery event no longer include your spouses or consorts; the event isn't designed with that possibility in mind, and having that be part of the sneaky lover discovery event feels better.
- Sufficiently sneaky lovers are now unlikely to be caught in seclusion.
- Decreased the chance of going Craven due to not opening the gates, but increased the chance of picking up other "fun" traits.
- Capped scaled_wealth in the Masque of the Red Death event chain.
- Small logic tweaks in the Masque of the Red Death event chain.
- Being suspicious of Death now moves you towards Paranoid, not towards Cynical.
- Death now vanishes without trace, as opposed to becoming a regular mortal.
- Compressed the timescale for the Masque of the Red Death party a bit.
- Reworked vanilla's food warning events and food mechanic into something more gradual and saner.
- Several new ways to gain or conserve food while in seclusion have been added, generally not very friendly. Those courtiers had it coming!
- Eating rats now harms your prestige.
- Refusing to eat rats no longer makes you jump directly from Humble to Proud.
- Mittens the Kitten and generic pet cats might now help with rat problems.
- Fixed some instances of set_defacto_liege that potentially were broken.
- The Children's Crusade will no longer recruit unbeliever children, including those with a secret religion.
- Children joining the Children's Crusade will now be flagged to not convert in AI hands.
- The restorer of the Roman Empire now gains a bloodline even if they start with a historical "Roman bloodline", seeing as it is still kind of a big deal.
- Made epic hangovers a bit more common in general.
- If the captured sneaker leads a very unsuccessful peasant invasion they will now be executed for their treason.
- Muslims partying in seclusion will now experience everyone's favourite vanilla mechanic: Decadence!
- Pacifists now lose piety for stuff like eating rats or engaging in cannibalism. Vanilla didn't see fit to bother with either...
- The AI can now get the Masque of the Red Death event chain if it is permitted supernatural events.
- Rats no longer particularly care whether you're an adult or incapable when eating your food.
- Heathens and heretics will no longer believe in your prayers if they've been proven ineffective.
- The EoC will now consider the gender of the vassal/courtier potentially being given a county when he gets one as a Tribute, based on the laws of e_china.
- The EoC will no longer give counties to his concubines, seeing as that makes them stop being concubines.
- Recreating China using the CI decision should now properly award a real CI bloodline.
- Overthrown CI emperors should no longer keep the Son of Heaven trait.
- The AI should now respect its primary title's gender law a bit more when picking a Crusade beneficiary.
- Added proper event pictures and borders for various new events.
- JD Master Engineers hanging out in a landless court (e.g. that of a Patrician or a merc company) will no longer build stuff in their "capital", seeing as that doesn't actually belong to their employer.
- JD Master Engineers need to be accessible and the like to build stuff.
- If the PR cannot be placed under their proper liege due to their respective tier they'll stop being the PR when the next attempt is made to place them under their proper liege.
- Even very tolerant characters are now permitted to execute and brand captured DWs; SRS members remain off the table.
- The AI will now always take part in Hwarang and RAS discussion flavour events, consistent with the WotRS ones.
- Fixed severa issues with RIP epidemic witch hunts.
- Improved RIP epidemic witch hunt AI logic.
- RIP epidemic witch hunt rivals are now, you know, actual rivals, as opposed to the game making two characters into rivals without checking whether they get along.
- RIP epidemic witch hunts no longer results in courtiers getting randomly spawned lovers "because drama". They instead involve someone with a suitable lover.
- RIP epidemic witch hunts concerning jealous lovers now use sane targets and sane scopes.
- Known Devil Worshipers and suspect ones are a bit more likely to be caught in witch hunts.
- Characters should no longer accuse their friends, lovers, or non-rival kins(wo)men of being witches.
- Burning witches that are kin now counts as kinslaying.
- You'll no longer get depressed if your rival relatives gets murdered for being a suspected witch.
- Relatives with sufficient Intrigue or PCS will no longer be randomly murdered in witch hunts.
- Split rip_flavor_events.txt into smaller, more focused, files.
- Removed Physician-Missionary events; they're poorly implemented, the logic doesn't match other missionaries, and salvaging them would be rather messy.
- Improved event picture setup for a few WotRS and Hwarang events.
- Immortality suspicion events now only fire for characters with the Immortal trait (as opposed to any "immortal = yes" trait).
- The Immortal Rival event chain can no longer spawn a "Pagan" religion character, seeing as that can result in various kinds of issues.
- Incitatus, Trampler of Nations, now has his nickname.
- Incitatus now goes after someone with the Horse trait rather than someone with Horse culture, seeing as the two are not necessarily the same.
- Incitatus now murders your horse, as opposed to merely executing them.
- Incitatus no longer kills incapable or imprisoned horses.
- Defeating an Immortal Rival now gives you some prestige.
- Defeating an Immortal Rival might have an effect on your traits now.
- The Immortal Rival event chain now has some AI logic (up from none whatsoever).
- The "hidden guard posts" trick for defeating an Immortal Rival is now something that's tied to high Martial rather than high Stewardship, seeing as it isn't at all about Stewardship.
- Hunters are no longer assumed to keep platypi on hand to feast on their Immortal Rivals.
- Lieges being actively asked for stuff for an immortality potion will not believe their Court Alchemist is slacking off at that very moment and will thus not be impatient right then.
- Lieges will now think a bit more highly of those actively brewing a (supposed) immortality potion for them, making it less likely they'll e.g. plot to murder the Court Alchemist.
- Paying for Death to stay at an inn now has a cap on the scaled_wealth; a fairly high one for a room at an inn, but still not uncapped.
- The Chess with Death event chain now has AI logic (vanilla didn't see fit to give it any).
- Game Masters are now better at chess.
- Partially restored the "Death ping" event in the Chess with Death event chain; without it, the loc breaks in some places.
- Death's sexuality no longer depends on your traits.
- While Death's reach of course is long, you are no longer permitted to offer up your vassals as sacrifices to Death.
- While funny, you're no longer able to offer your rivals as sacrifices to Death when he offers to restore your captured Rook.
- Improved AI logic for vassal meddling events a bit; the vanilla logic is mostly good, for once!
- Capped scaled_wealth to be paid to vassals when meddling.
- Vassals are now always pacified for three years when their wars are meddled with, as opposed to sometimes being pacified for a lesser amount of time.
- The giving_me_immortality_potion persistent_event_target is now cleared up in various places where it should be.
- Immortality potion outcomes (if the potion is fully consumed) will now have potion/poison skills, with the specifics depending on the outcome.
- RIP mourning events now have some more AI logic.
- AI RIP mourners will no longer befriend their rivals or lovers; the former is certainly not a friend, the latter is presumably not someone you're not already close to.
- RIP mourners will no longer become more Zealous due to hanging out with priests if they've got a secret religion.
- RIP mourning events will now notify you of trait changes when they should.
- Put some society-related random_lists inside a hidden_tooltip.
- RIP execution nickname logic is now a bit saner.
- Made even more events use proper effects for adding/removing traits.
- Incitatus, Trampler of Nations, is now quite a bit younger, seeing as he presumably wasn't born well before the founding of Rome. He's now assumed to be have been a young adult when Caligula came to power.
- Telling your chancellor that you've not interest in talking to foreigners that don't speak your language can no longer instantly move you from Humble to Proud or from Gregarious to Shy; that's a bit extreme.
- Courtiers being given nicknames should now get less silly nicknames.
- Antagonize targets no longer get to challenge you to a duel if you or they are at war or you or they are busy(/incapable/inaccessible/imprisoned), seeing as that can result in very silly things.
- Hermetics and RAS members will no longer infiltrate the supposed labs of adventurers (raiding or otherwise), seeing as they've left their homes and presumably labs behind.
- Hedge Knights now only visit feudals.
- Disfigured characters that are rather unlikely to be looking for love(rs) should no longer get events about that.
- Dead or incapable/inaccessible/imprisoned spouses should no longer tell Disfigured characters you're scaring the children.
- Incapable/inaccessible/imprisoned children should no longer be scared by Disfigured parents.
- Adolescent children no longer pick up Timid from Disfigured parents; they instead move towards Craven.
- Disfigured characters hosting a masquerade ball no longer invite vassals that are unable to participate, e.g. due to being in seclusion.
- Vassals of vassals no longer care about insanity at a masquerade ball they're not attending.
- Vassals of vassals no longer care about lack of insanity at a masquerade ball they're not attending.
- A certain RAS-related event modifier now prevents the One-Eyed flavour events. Its owner would not approve.
- Characters that are unlikely to engage in duels are no longer going to try to become martially ambidextrous.
- Turning down the opportunity to become martially ambidextrous prevents you from getting that event chain again, seeing as it's otherwise a way to get +1 to Learning multiple times.
- One-legged characters assumed not to be looking for love(rs) should no longer dance with someone.
- One-legged characters dancing with someone will no longer gain a lover even if they do well; hard to salvage that event chain in another way since it doesn't notify the target, doesn't prevent them from being landed, *and* doesn't explicitly claim there was tumbling.
- One-eyed character will now be a bit upset with a child that takes out their second eye with a particularly well-aimed rock.
- Rock-throwing children are now a bit more sensibly picked.
- RAS and Hermetic events involving standard ingredient selection should now always clear selection flags on old ingredients before selecting new ones, in case something odd has happened.
- Avoiding a thrown rock is no longer going to shift you directly from Brave to Craven.
- Added AI logic for the RIP maimed flavour events; vanilla had very little.
- The Child of Destiny will no longer invade their friends or lovers, at least not in the initial war.
- Adventurers will no longer invade their friends or lovers, at least not in the initial war.
- Raiding adventurers should no longer fail to launch their war because they initially only could pick the Tenno/knockoff and then realized that they were unable to target those at the last minute.
- The Merry Men's presence should no longer cause extreme personality changes (read: You will no longer shift fully from one extreme to another in any one of those events).
- Feudal life events are now more restrictive government-wise (generally no nomads, sometimes further restrictions on top of that).
- Women can now catch their villagers trying to burn suspected witches (huge scales and ducks not included).
- Nobles being kidnapped should now be, you know, nobles.
- Married vassals explicitly referred to as male will now be selected in a fashion that actually requires them to be male.
- Bards no longer show up in weird parts of the map in feudal life events.
- Feudal life events no longer cause extreme personality swings.
- Feudal life events now notify you about personality trait changes.
- RIP prosperity flavour events now use capped scaled_wealth, seeing as it really isn't worth invesing in a province for a two year modifier if it costs a ton.
- Lowered AI wealth requirements for RIP prosperity flavour modifiers; 300 gold is pretty high.
- Added AI logic for RIP prosperity flavour (vanilla had no trait weights).
- Children taking care of you while you are sick now need to be adults.
- AI characters taking care of you while you are sick will now consider being friends/lovers/rivals blockers (in different fashions) for some options.
- AI characters no longer decide on caretakers based on a coin flip.
- You are now allowed to be happy over a newborn daughter that's the heir apparent due to gender laws.
- You are no longer allowed to be happy over a newborn son that's not the heir apparent due to gender laws.
- You now get to be disappointed with a firstborn son because he's not a girl if you've got Enatic, as opposed to just being disappointed with firstborn daughters under Agnatic.
- Children whose parent told them they were similar to a grandparent while dying no longer think of the dungeons for some reason.
- Babies no longer understand whatever their parents said while dying and, those too young to read can't very well go hunting for a diary.
- Rulers whose parent(s) died very early in their life no longer remember them well enough to tell their own children they're similar to said parent(s).
- If the grandparent that died no longer possesses the current capital they can't very well write about buried valuables there.
- Dead grandparents now hide a flat amount of money, considering their grandkids could have a significantly higher income and that they therefore shouldn't have left something based on said grandkid's scaled_wealth.
- Characters that don't believe in reincarnation no longer move towards Cynical if they decide that their newborn child certainly isn't a reincarnation.
- Taoists and those that reformed with access to the RAS no longer care about Immortals ruling over them.
- A Kiyohara victory in the "Northern Disturbance" no longer makes them Japanese Feudal, seeing as they start as that and that you only have yourself to blame if you switch away from it (the AI wouldn't bother to).
- Fixed an issue where people defecting to the old EoC didn't actually defect as they should.
- Further tweaks to AI willingness to go completely independent on a CI dynasty change in China if not able to found a CI empire.
- Replaced many instances of GFX_evt_stressed_ruler with GFX_evt_bad_news.
- Religious minority communities now last 50 years.
- Religious minority communities are now Silk Road minority communities where appropriate.

- SRS communities now last 50 years (down from "Eternally unless the province flips (or the community becomes public)").
- Chinese and Japanese government lieges and characters now always frown on sacrifices of human beings in ancestor worship events, and will not permit them.
- Several of the "interesting Muslims" are now Renowned Physicians", because they were.

- Set up blockers for people on/about to depart on the Penglai Expedition.
- Set up Chinese zodiac/element handout events.
- Killing kin as part of burninating a province with extreme prejudice now results in kinslayer traits.

- Freckles are no longer removed for "vision babies".
- Expecting mothers that promise to live a celibate life no longer stop caring about living a *chaste* life.
- Minor tweaks to HFP_pregnancy_events, mainly trait notifications and preventing stacking of stat boosts from the same event.
- Dead eunuchs' children are not automatically assumed to be illegitimate.
- Homosexual women that have been caught sleeping with other women are no longer assumed to be giving birth to bastards just because their husband has died.
- Normans and North Germanics no longer become less adventure-happy because a magical date has passed if you start before said magical date.
- True religion now matters when the AI decides whether it believes in reincarnation.

- Randomly Jewish physicians are now less common for Chinese culture group characters that don't also belong to a religion group that makes that likely.

- Credited immortality potion-related deaths to the alchemist.

- Zealots will no longer accept mystic treatments from heathens or heretics (checking both public and secret religion of the zealot).
- Unimportant characters will no longer ask terrible physicians to try risky experimental things unless they are stupid.
- Court Physicians now get to specifically pick their treatment within the category their liege chooses.

- Physicians may no longer sacrifice adult children of the person being treated.

- Experimental treatments now work as they should.

- If you drink a non-working Immortality Potion provided by your Court Alchemist (or one that you brewed yourself) it now hurts your MoH (at the end of the event chain).

Decisions:
- Blocked some society-related decisions for incapable/inaceessible/imprisoned Hermetics; for example, good job brewing a potion from a jail cell.
- Set up society power decisions for the Renowned Apothecary Society society.
- Added decision for Court Alchemists to ask their liege for ingredients.
- Added targeted decisions for Chinese government lieges to ask for various potions (including immortality potions) from their Court Alchemist.
- Added targeted decisions to ask a friend/lover (that's in the RAS) in the same realm for various potions (excluding immortality potions).
- Added targeted decision for Court Alchemists to ask their liege if they maybe can offer a prisoner their freedom in exchange for helping with a scientific experiment.
- Immortals (and Kitsune) can no longer request Immortality Potions or brew them for themselves, seeing as they have no need for them.
- Immortals and Kitsune may no longer drink from the Fountain of Youth, seeing as the new setup wouldn't work for them.
- You now need a practical_age of 18 or greater to drink from the Fountain of Youth, rather than an age of 18 or older.
- Characters with a liege that's fine with other religions (e.g. a nomadic liege) no longer get to start SRSes; reduces the amount of nonsense in China/Japan/etc.
- Christian characters with a Christian liege only get to found SRSes if they consider their liege a heretic; potentially something to revise a bit, but preventing the AI from stupiding itself into a prison cell with a liege that wouldn't care about their secret religion if it was public is good.
- Dharmics no longer get to found SRSes if they have a Dharmics liege, seeing as they're normally fine with coexisting and the AI otherwise does silly things.
- Slightly opened up the availability of the Phalaris bloodline.
- If you've got an actual regency you're no longer allowed to torture/eat/etc. your prisoners; results in a saner setup.
- Made nicknames from hostile prisoner interactions rarer, and added some triggers to prevent more silly choices (e.g. Dragon culture characters becoming "the Dragon").
- Torturing/etc. your prisoners no longer removes imprisonment reasons if you're using the game rule that doesn't force you to release them, resulting in a more consistent approach to that sort of thing.
- "Chinese enough" and Japonic characters no longer necessarily associate dragons with burning people to death.
- Non-Lunatic/-Possessed AI characters will no longer read poetry to prisoners if it'd release them and they have reasons to keep them imprisoned.
- The AI is now less likely to humiliate dangerous prisoners if doing so would release them.
- Cannibals may now eat prisoners even if they're not also Possessed/Lunatics.
- Removed the "Crazy Cannibal" modifier; replaced with the perfectly good Cannibal trait.
- You are no longer prevented from removing your prisoner's remaining eye if they are One-Eyed; blinding them is certainly still on the table!
- Blinding prisoners through normal torturing now helps Greeks towards being known for their hatred of sunlight, consistent with specific prisoner blinding.
- Pacifists now lose some more piety for certain hostile acts against prisoners.
- Hovering over the "Found Heian-kyo" decision should no longer crash the game.
- The "Found Heian-kyo" decision will now be properly disabled if the barony in question is constructed normally from the 769 start and later ends up destroyed.
- Neither Cairo nor Heian-kyo can be constructed by decision if the province in question already is constructing a holding, preventing some possible edge case oddities.
- Restricted "Land for hospital" HO deals to non-pagans and Cosmopolitan/Peaceful/Tolerant pagans; it's a bit unreasonable to assume all pagans HOs would also be building hospitals...
- The EoC can no longer release tributaries by decision; that sort of thing needs proper integration with the Grace system/Chinese mechanics.
- Slightly adjusted Recruit Prisoner conditions, making it harder to snipe heirs.
- Made the conditions to meddle in vassal wars saner.
- Made the conditions to demand Gavelkind saner.
- Made the requirements for "Ask to Leave Court" slightly stricter.
- "Land for hospital" HO deals can no longer be entered into if you have a secret religion.
- "Land for hospital" HO deals are now unavailable if any liege has a different religion.
- You can no longer exchange land for a hospital if you have expelled the HO in question.
- "Demand Gavelkind" (both versions) now forces the target to change one primary tier title that is not under Gavelkind to Gavelkind, preferring secondary non-rel head titles.
- Constructing the Third Temple will now create a special Great Synagogue in Jerusalem if possible should wonder construction be permitted, and the once-delayed narrative event now fires right away (after the personal character event) instead of being delayed; considered a vanilla oversight fix.
- Prisoner mutilation now tries to pick something that will be particularly harsh or fitting for the specific prisoner; for example, if you are a man who slept with someone's wife and got caught, say goodbye to your "favourite toy".
- If you mutilate someone in a certain way, they are fairly likely to respond in kind if they decide to mutilate you; an eye for an eye!
- Added a decision to clear out the guy with the funny hat's palace to make way for bread and circuses.
- Added a new bloodline decision for those with one of the new Roman bloodlines.
- Atlanteans can now Delve into the Classics.
- If the Myrmidons are known as the Amazons, they will no longer get a male leader at the start.
- If you go into seclusion and your Court Physician is not your courtier they will now be fired so that you can appoint someone that's in seclusion with you, seeing as a Court Physician living elsewhere won't be much help.
- Tribal rulers are now able to go into seclusion, seeing as there doesn't seem to be a lot of reason to exclude them. Some event chains are unavailable for them, however.
- Fixed some silliness and a possible edge case related to nomadic settling. Truly the kind of gift that keeps giving, that nomadic government...
- Added a decision for Tibetan Buddhists without a Chinese government to convert to the Bön religion (unreformed or reformed depends on whether it has been reformed). Should probably have checks regarding whether the Bön religion is close enough to the Buddhist religion added at some point.
- Added a decision for Tibetan Bön characters to convert to the Buddhist religion. Should probably have checks regarding whether the Bön religion is close enough to the Buddhist religion added at some point.
- Added a new "Impose Quarantine" title decision to temporarily boost the disease resistance of a province. Comes with some side-effects, so building a hospital is generally better.
- Added an AI version of the "Impose Quarantine" decision that works for their whole demesne, in order to not have it check each title.
- You are no longer allowed to give your public religion's holy sites, your secret religion's holy sites, the province owner's public holy sites, or any liege of the province owner's holy sites to raiding adventurers. Vanilla stupidity.
- Made the AI considerably more interested in building hospitals.
- Minor tweaks to seppuku and jigaki decisions.
- Tribals and nomads need no longer apply for restroring the Roman Empire, seeing as that can result in odd things.
- Added a new decision related to the Penglai Expedition.
- Added restrictions related to the Penglai Expedition.
- Alcohol gifts can no longer be given to people that are inaccessible, incapable, or the like.
- Incapable characters no longer care about your scary wonder upgrades.
- If you are about to depart with the Treasure Fleet or are preparing one of those you are now rather too busy to introduce your heir to the realm.
- Characters with a secret religion can no longer fund the religious ceremonies of their public religion.
- Warrior monks of various stripes are now disinclined to train those about to embark on a voyage.
- If you are about to embark on a voyage you can no longer request to become the Grand Chancellor, seeing as you'll need to resign very quickly.
- If you are about to embark on a voyage you can't decide to take a quick detour to deal with a courtier/eunuch problem at court.
- If you are preparing a Treasure Fleet you can no longer instead focus on dealing with a courtier/eunuch problem at court with extreme prejudice.
- Further adjustments to AI hospital construction willingness.
- Restricted Constantinople takeovers for holders of e_byzantium to those with the proper government type.
- Only those with the proper government type get to move the Roman capital to Rome.
- Only those with the proper government type get to found the Varangian Guard.
- You no longer get to carry out a plot-based revoke on a vassal title when on a voyage or preparing one.
- DWs can no longer engage in recruitment or desecration while on or preparing a voyage.
- Tiger hunts are now incompatible with Treasure Fleet or Penglai activities
- Chinese and Japanese government lieges now frown on bloodthirsty mass sacrifices, blood games, Aztec sacrifices, generic Bloodthirsty sacrifices, and Great Blots, and will not permit them.
- Monastic seclusion now has some sane limits.
- If none of your vassals are eligible to come to court to "help" with a eunuch/courtier problem you no longer get to attempt to get one of them to come there to "help" with the aforementioned problem.
- Set up some trigger checks related to nickname coolness.
- Making a Chinese princess available for regular marriage now comes with a Grace penalty, seeing as you might mess with the EoC's plans.
- Added a decision for Taoist divination (requires MNM; all but a copy-paste of Hermetic scrying).
- Seppuku and Jigaki conditions loosened a bit.
- A character eligible for Seppuku or Jigaki can no longer choose to commit some less honorable form of suicide.
- You can no longer send alcohol gifts while inaccessible, incapable, or imprisoned.
- The Hanami can no longer be held if inaccessible, incapable, or imprisoned.
- The Iyomante can no longer be held if inaccessible, incapable, or imprisoned.
- The Bear Festival can no longer be held if inaccessible, incapable, or imprisoned.
- You should now be able to seek treatment from a Court Physician secluded with you.
- The proper liege is now checked and contacted when it comes to requests to test potions on your liege's prisoners.
- Favour-related pardons now clear more relevant modifiers.
- Concubines offered to the EoC can no longer be very maimed.
- New spouses you select for the Tenno/etc. can no longer be Blinded (on top of old requirements).
- Going into Seclusion now hurts your MoH if you are Chinese Imperial, seeing as you clearly have no confidence in your ability to keep the realm safe from the disease outbreak.
- Cleared up irrelevant flag related to the Third Temple.
- Building the Third Temple now requires wonder construction to be permitted.
- The correct flag is now checked for the "Convert to Yupaychaspa" holy site decision (both reformed and unreformed).
- A couple of TF-related flags are now properly set (and later cleared).
- Roman legions are now as limited as intended.
- Fixed incorrect flag used for Imperial Claimant factions.
- Removed unused "feudalize_handled_capital" flag.
- Eating a pagan rel head now sets the correct flag.
- The Jashan-e-sadah now set the proper flag.
- You may now only fund religious ceremonies once while attempting to strengthen your religion, as intended.
- Fixed incorrect flag being used to check if a character is at someone else's Diwali.
- Fixed incorrect flag being used to check if a character is at someone else's Zun festival.
- Nomadic raiding adventurers can now recruit troops as they should.
- The AI will no longer spam hospitals across its demesne all at once.
- You no longer need to care if the concubine you're offering to the EoC wants to keep it in the family, but whether you'd like to do that matters if they're a close relative.
- Fixed a couple of scopes in settling decisions for nomads.
- Crusade beneficiaries need to not be imprisoned to be selected (and to get land in the end).
- Abandoning the unreformed version of a pagan religion no longer makes courtiers and unplayable vassals follow along if they're Zealous (and aren't true believers secretly following the reformed religion). The Old Ways are still strong, as far as these people are concerned.
- Fixed nomadic setup for cross-religion seduction.
- Fixed extent of culture conversion of dynasty members on construction of Heian-kyo by decision.
- Fixed setup for nick_the_tyrant handout in some execution-related decisions.
- The Varangian Guard can no longer be founded if the Norse have reformed; they're rather less enticed by your civilized ways in that case.
- Made AI children much more prone to start delayed mourning by using the decision if eligible.

Should k_israel exist, *that* gets to be Judea; I'm sure the People's Front of Judea is pleased, even if the Romans certainly won't go home!

Succession voting:
- Glitterhoof now votes with their master.
- Mittens now sees fit to reward their servant by voting for their candidate.
- Minor tweaks to the ERE/RE's succession law to hook in bloodline weights.
- Roman Imperial Succession now has massive penalties towards electing anyone that'd change the succession law due to not being eligible for the correct government type.
- Decided that penalizing the appointment of sycophants or discharging of heroes eternally was silly, particularly considering immortality is a thing, and that 25 years is sufficient.
- The Tenno (and the Ryukyuan knockoff) is no longer somehow an eligible candidate in various elections that really don't concern him, e.g. the election for Hardrada's successor.

Traits:
- Added "Drank from the Fountain of Youth" trait.
- incapable is now an opposite of incapable_according_to_regent; the former should override the latter.
- Blinded is now an opposite of One-Eyed, meaning adding the former to someone with the latter removes the latter.
- Inverted the attraction opinion for Grievously Scarred and Horrifically Scarred; feels like a vanilla mistake, seeing as the amount of scars has moved from "That's rather dashing!" to "What happened to your face?".
- Increased the Immortal trait to +20 Health to ensure the severe symptom modifiers won't overcome it when stacked with normal symptoms.
- The Viking and Pirate traits and their upgrades are no longer cleared out if you convert religion/culture (they're still generally only earnable by the relevant religions/cultures).
- The Tenno should no longer keep Japanese government or regent traits if he has them prior to coming into possession of the Chrysanthemum Throne.
- Child of Concubine/Consort is now considered opposites of Bastard/Legitimate Bastard; if the child is a bastard, the other parent isn't a concubine/consort in CK2 terms.
- Immortals, Kitsune, and fellow drinkers from the Fountain of Youth can now see if someone has had a drink from said fountain.
- Added Chinese Zodiac and element traits. Inherent modifiers likely to be tweaked a bit down the line.
- Chinese Zodiac/Element traits can no longer be picked in the Ruler Designer.
- Western Zodiac traits can no longer be picked in the Ruler Designer.
- Added traits related to the Penglai Expedition.
- Bastard/Legit Bastard being the opposite of Child of Concubine/Consort now goes both ways.

Death reasons:
- Added new death reason tied to immortality potions.
- Added a few new suicide-related death reasons.
- Added two more murder death reasons and hooked them into various events.

Laws:
- The Vassal War Laws now use vassal_opinion rather than feudal_opinion, seeing as the latter makes foreign feudals upset.
- Fixed an issue with the gender laws file.
- Took yet further steps to prevent True Cognatic randomly being the law in Chinese-/Japanese-style realms.
- Tolerant religions no longer need Religious title revocation in order to enact Imperial Administration.
- Bureaucratic governments (CI, EI, ConBu, JI, DI, JB) no longer need Religious title revocation in order to enact Imperial Administration.
- Bureaucratic governments (CI, EI, ConBu, JI, DI, JB) no longer need to depower the council in order to enact Imperial Administration.
- The Nicene and Trapezuntine Empires now have Imperial Elective if eligible for the Roman Imperial government type.

Governments:
- The Nicene and Trapezuntine Empires now have the Roman Imperial government type if held by a feudal ruler ineligible for a Chinese or Japanese-style government.
- All feudal (is_feudal = yes) governments are now set to only prefer castle holdings; prevents the AI from potentially doing some weird things. This does not change which holdings they are permitted to hold.

Religion features:
- Added a new "Appreciated Alchemists" Doctrine, unlocking access to the RAS and the Court Alchemist minor title.
- You're now forbidden from becoming the rel head, reforming in a Dogmatic/Proselytizing/Unyielding fashion, or picking Religious Tax or a Doctrine that contains it should you reform while you have a secret religion, seeing as those are all actively detrimental to your true religion (or inherently silly, in the case of becoming a rel head). Fully blocking reformations if you have a secret religion unfortunately causes a reproduceable CTD.
- Removed some has_selected_religion_feature logic the Validator didn't like.

Nicknames:
- Greeks may now sometimes pick up the "Philadelphos" nickname should the circumstances warrant it.
- Hopefully fixed a bug where the wrong party's deities were used for the "the Sword of <Deity>" nickname.
- Added new "the Cuman" nickname.
- You no longer nickname people "the Fairest/Strongest/etc." of whichever random province you happen to be passing through at the moment.
- Added the "the Bent" nickname.
- Added "Strongbow" nickname.

Religions:
- Removed the Taoist religion's +2 to Stewardship; it has always been odd, and we might as well get it out of the way.
- Flagged the Mazdaki religion as pacifist, because of Mazdak's teachings putting an emphasis on it and on being a vegetarian and not killing animals, which is pretty much how the Jain religion's setup works in that regard.
- Karaite men can now marry up to four wives, seeing as the religion has never outlawed polygamy to this very day. Seeing as the wives are supposed to be treated as equal, concubinage is a worse setup.

Game rules:
- Added a new game rule governing whether the AI can meddle in vassal wars/demand Gavelkind, seeing as letting the AI do so isn't in line with vanilla.

Wonders and wonder upgrades:
- Atlanteans now gain access to Grand Amphitheater-type Wonders.
- Added Third Temple Wonder (a slightly cheaper non-generic Great Synagogue).
- Added Colosseum Wonder (a slightly cheaper non-generic Grand Amphitheater with more restrictive upgrades to ensure it doesn't become a silly place).
- Unreformed pagans can now make use of any Great (Pagan) Temple they take control of, but they are still not allowed to build completely new ones.
- RAS members can now build Herb Gardens.
- Added new Wing of Medicine upgrade for Grand Universities.
- Added new Medicine and Pharmacology Collection upgrade for Great Libraries and the House of Wisdom.
- The Jewish religions can no longer build a regular Great Synagogue in Jerusalem.
- Fixed some flag issues.

Terrain and province static modifiers:
- Added some disease resistance boosts/penalties to some different terrain types.
- Added some disease resistance boosts during winter.
- Added a disease resistance penalty in coastal provinces.

CoAs:
- Added new flag for e_nicaea.
- Added flag for e_trebizond.
- Added a new CoA for England prior to the Norman Yoke being on English neck.

- Added new CoA for k_armenia, seeing as the old one now is used for k_cilician_armenia.
- Added CoA for k_karashar; nothing fancy, but...

Objectives:
- Fixed some issues with the claimant faction logic.
- It is no longer possible to be in a "Create Permanent Regency" faction if your liege is fighting to end an ongoing Permanent Regency, seeing as that can result in two Permanent Regents of the same kind, which possibly results in weird things.
- Fabrication plots will now cancel if the plotter stops being in the relevant de jure.
- The AI is now more likely to select a focus if it has a mission to select that focus.
- Fixes to claimant factions in China/etc.
- Added a new faction related to the Penglai Expedition.
- Factions are now properly blocked for those on/preparing/about to embark on a Penglai Expedition.
- Commented out a couple of achievement-related checks.
- The non-WoL "Improve <Attribute>" ambitions can now only be completed once, as was probably intended considering the flag.
- Fixed missing flag check in GC bloodline ambition.
- Removed unused flag from "Lower Decadence" ambition.
- Fixed missing flags for seizing trade post plots.
- The correct flag is now set when finishing the Great Work construction-related ambition.

- Fixed a CleanSlate issue with the Zealot voting pattern's war declaration logic.

Building:
