Beta 14.0.1 - 29/01/2024
--------------------------------------

IMPORTANT: Tianxia has now been rebased off of CleanSlate; CleanSlate 1.0 Beta 6.1 [EZKM] (https://github.com/ck2plus/CleanSlate/releases/tag/1.0-beta6.1) MUST be active for Tianxia to run.
IMPORTANT: Any old Tianxia.mod file MUST be replaced with the new one; the new version contains important changes, such as actually setting up CleanSlate as a dependency.
IMPORTANT: Delete any old Tianxia directory; don't just overwrite it with the new one.
IMPORTANT: Pre-14.0.0 saves will not work, 14.0.0 saves are likely to experience some oddities; starting a new game would be advisable.
IMPORTANT: Random/Shattered world and the EU4 Converter are not supported.
IMPORTANT: Start dates after 1127.4.20 remain unsupported; it's possible to start in them, but they're not expected to be in a good spot, and are not a priority to fix.

# History
- Historical characters with higher tier scar traits should now get proper scars.
- Shotoku's council is no longer fully depowered, meaning she won't pretty much immediately block internal vassal wars.
- Fixed Honorius' death reason.
- Fixed Minamoto Yoshitomo's death reason.
- Fixes to a couple of Cham rulers.
- Added nicknames for a bunch of historical rulers (and some other characters).
- Some date and trait tweaks to historical characters, because Paradox didn't necessarily care. Lots of weirdness remains, of course, because vanilla has a lot of questionable history...
- Historical characters in need of an armour stretcher should now look the part.
- Historical Romans now know to use "Hilarius" rather than "Hilary".
- With the sole exception of Herakleios there are no longer any characters awarded the "Crusader" trait ahead of the start of the First Crusade, unlike what vanilla thinks is reasonable.
- While the Childrens' Crusade was a thing, various historical characters that have nothing to do with that incident no longer gain the "Crusader" trait on birth. Again, vanilla being silly...
- Historical Saio/Saiin are now referred to as such.
- Improved the traits of Queen Sundeok.
- Kublai now becomes a Poet partway through his life.
- Fixed a couple of add_lover effects outside effect blocks.
- Disinherited historical characters in Japan no longer gain/lose the Bastard trait alongside the Disinherited trait.

# Religions and religion features
- Lowered Shinto Intrigue boost to +2 (was +3).
- Lowered Koshin Diplomacy boost to +2 (was +3).
- The unique Bimoist feature is now actually unique to them, as opposed to being both valid and highly rated for everyone.
- Unreformed Tengri and Jurchen pagans now have Defensive Attrition, seeing as there's no good reason for them not to have it.
- Unreformed Aztec and Yupaychaspa pagans now ignore Defensive Attrition.
- Hellenics now remember that the name "Alexander" (and its cultural variants) is a perfectly valid name; a great name, some might even say!

# Decisions
- It is now possible to Sway your rel head if they're non-Theocratic.
- It is no longer possible to falsely claim to belong to your liege's faith by decision if your liege doesn't care about religion, thus somewhat lowering the chance of SRSes where they don't make sense.
- It is no longer possible to ask to swap a child to the Heritage focus if their educator doesn't share your culture and religion.
- It is no longer possible to ask to swap a child to the Faith focus if their educator doesn't share your religion.
- It is no longer possible to ask to swap a child to the Heritage focus if you shouldn't care, e.g. if only the religion is mismatched and you're a nomad. Proud characters are excepted as far as both religion and culture goes, Zealous as far as religion goes.
- It is no longer possible to ask to swap a child to the Faith focus if you shouldn't care, e.g. if you're a nomad. Zealous characters are the exception
- Vassals can no longer offer to become tributaries of China. Sorry, Shimazu; you'll have to figure out a better trick with Ryukyu!
- Incapable, inaccessible, and/or imprisoned characters can no longer start composing books.
- The decision to mourn previously unmourned ancestors is now a high priority decision.
- The lower percentage plot fabrication decisions are no longer available if the higher percentage ones can be taken, seeing as there's absolutely no reason to take the worse one.
- Imperial Guard regiments can now only be raised if your realm size is greater or equal to 50.
- Raising a second Imperial Guard regiment now requires a realm size of 100 or more, and each regiment beyond the second requires another 100 realm size.
- WotRS members can now advance past the rank of Novice without proving themselves to a mentor, though only if ten years have passed since they joined the society.
- Player WotRS members of Samurai/Onna-bugeisha rank or above can now properly offer to mentor WotRS Novices; the blocker now correctly prevents the AI from offering to mentor the player, as opposed to preventing the player from targeting the AI.
- The FotK power to switch to Japanese Monastic Feudal is now properly allowed for Japanese Feudal player characters.
- The FotK power to switch to Japanese Monastic Feudal is now properly disallowed for Japanese Monastic Feudal player characters.
- It is no longer possible to ask your liege for a title while your liege is at war, bringing the decision in line with the AI pulse event.
- Made the AI less eager to found and refound Imperial Guard regiments.
- Increased the cost of founding, refounding, and retaking control of Imperial Guard regiments.
- Characters flagged to never convert should no longer convert to an attacker's religion in AI hands. They have chosen their hill, and they *will* die on it!
- Characters that has no ancestor to mourn no longer see the decision to mourn.
- Characters that alreayd are mourning no longer see the decision to mourn.
- Characters with a Dogmatic religion are now only valid seduction targets for co-religionists.
- The decisions to increase/decrease Regency Power are now considered high priority decisions by default.
- Taoists and Confucians no longer get to borrow from the Jews.
- AI DWs will now be rather less interested in Demonic Possession against non-quest targets.
- You now need to wait one month between each Grace interaction, to ensure that the previous one properly resolves.
- It is no longer possible to request a Chinese artefact if the EoC can't grant you any additional ones at his current MoH rating.
- Fixed an issue where eunuch tributes might not be properly snipped or properly shipped off to China.
- Descriptions for many Grace interactions no longer overflow and no longer claim characters that aren't disinherited will be disinherited.
- Tributaries of CI realms may no longer break free by decision unless their suzerain has a sub-Average MoH rating; this helps ensure Song doesn't break the Treaty of Chanyuan very early, and helps Liao a bit.
- Made the AI more eager to go Japanese Feudal at higher tiers, hopefully making Japan more likely to slide towards a more feudal setup.

# Graphics and interface
- The "In mourning" trait icon should now be visible, as opposed to being invisible.
- Fixed Shinto/etc. dynasty CoA misalignment, at least partially.
- Hopefully fixed all black holding pictures.

# Cultures
- Tanguts now use tangutgfx if the "Tianxia Jade Dragon Portraits Expansion" ("Tianxia: Chinese, Khmer, & Tibetan Portraits" in the launcher) or another source of tangutgfx portraits is active. Note that the aforementioned graphical submod has been updated after 14.0.0's release, fixing an issue where all adult tangutgfx women looked positively ancient.
- Corrected spelling of Tsuhime; was "Tsuhgime", which of course can't be proper as it can't be spelled out in Hiragana!
- Sakahito and Sadatojo are no longer considered female Yamato names.
- Atlantean culture now has random = no.

# Landed titles
- All Tianxia merc companies now have short_name = yes.
- The Teutonic State now has Marienburg as its capital.

# Artefacts
- Fixed the name and tag of the Danjing Yaojue.
- The Wuzi no longer has the Art of War effect.
- The Lessons for Women is now a bit better than the Womens' Analects.

# Casus belli
- MoH rebels that end up with very small realms will no longer see fit to claim the Mandate of Heaven.
- Wars to Maintain the Permanent Regency no longer end on the death of the Permanent Regent.
- The AI will now execute peasant/etc. revolt leaders if they defeat the revolt, thus removing some characters that are unlikely to add anything but a performance drain.
- The Chinese and Japanese Reconquest CBs should now only be visible on independent targets that are not your top liege.
- Winning an Imperial Claimant faction war now partially empowers the council and shifts the vassal war laws one step in the vassals' favour unless the faction leader becomes the new Tenno.
- The Grand Chancellor can now use the Chinese Subjugation CB regardless of their tier if fully empowered.
- Vassals can now use the Chinese Subjugation CB regardless of their tier if the interest group is fully empowered.
- Wars vs. the PR to take over the position as PR should no longer instantly invalidate.
- The War of Honor CB should now be visible as expected, as opposed to always being invisible.
- The Rising Sun Subjugation CB should now be visible as expected, as opposed to always being invisible.
- The Ryukyuan Subjugation CB should now be visible as expected, as opposed to always being invisible.
- Optimised some things.
- Hid several CBs for people that won't be eligible for them without e.g. conversion.
- County-tier adventurer conquests no longer give the conqueror a nickname, seeing as it's not particularly impressive.

# Traits
- The following traits are no longer possible to select in the Ruler Designer: Son/Daughter of Heaven, Former Son/Daughter of Heaven, Incapable according to regent.
- The following traits are no longer randomly handed out: Son/Daughter of Heaven, Former Son/Daughter of Heaven
- The Kitsune trait can now be selected in the Ruler Designer.
- The various non-Tribal Kinslayer traits are no longer removed on becoming tribal. They will instead be converted to Tribal Kinslayer on_startup, which should help ensure that some historical Kinslayers' deeds aren't forgotten about.

# Bloodlines
- Confucius' bloodline now allows for faster conversion to Confucianism.

# Laws and succession voting
- Attempted to fix Japanese and Chinese characters randomly deciding that women maybe should inherit.
- Each Regency Power law enacted in favour of the Permanent Regent now enacts one Council Power law in favour of the Council if the "same number" Council Power law is in the liege's favour, meaning that the liege starts out less powerful if the Permanent Regency ends.
- Fixed CleanSlate-derived bug where you weren't allowed to switch *to* Primogeniture if your liege can grant viceroyalties and you aren't a viceroy; as per the comment, the effect now is that non-Muslim viceroys *can* switch *to* primogeniture even if they wouldn't be allowed to if not a viceroy.
- Dai culture now unlocks access to Ultimogeniture (if not blocked for some reason, e.g. due to government type).
- Minahasan culture electors no longer care about the candidate's gender.

# Dynasties
- The mysterious "L??" dynasties have been renamed.

# Nicknames
- Added a bunch of new historical nicknames (and a few derived from historical ones).
- The "the Devil" nickname now has more sane restrictions.
- Louis the Blind is no longer born Blinded or with his nickname; his blinding has been moved to the proper date.

# Ambitions
- The "Become Immortal" ambition now cancels if you stop being a Taoist.
- The "Acquire Title" Ambition should now actually complete if the liege gives you a title through the relevant event, as opposed to being impossible to complete.
- It is now possible to retain the "Groom an Heir" Ambition after passing certain practical age thresholds if you have a living child.
- Celibate children now count for "Groom an Heir"; the trait isn't an inheritance blocker!

# Factions
- The AI will now be more eager to start Create Permanent Regency and Proclaim Shogunate factions if their liege has depowered the council, in whole or in part, though only if there isn't a Permanent Regency going on.
- Various bureaucrats are now more fond of increasing council power.
- Many faction-related instances of is_primary_holder_title have been replaced with is_primary_holder_title_tier; it changes vanilla, but it's odd not to be able to press your rightful claims on secondary titles just because your liege is a duke.
- Members of Jimmu's bloodline are now more likely to start Shogunate factions.
- Members of Hachimantaro's bloodline are now more likely to start Shogunate factions.

# Focuses
- All (adult) Focuses are now invalid for prisoners, seeing as leaving the Seduction focus available means the AI frequently goes "I am a prisoner, so I should pick Seduction!" and then goes on a Seduction rampage if freed. It changes vanilla, but vanilla's implementation is insane.

# Event modifiers
- Maidservant Lover and Bodyguard Lover modifiers are now only visible for the character that seduced them, as opposed to being visible to everyone, including possibly spouses.
- Religious trading communities now have a better icon.

# Societies
- Increased Hwarang joining age limit to 35 (was 30).
- Aside from the Tenno and the Ryukyuan rel head (regardless of reformation), the WotRS no longer permit rel heads to join.
- The WotRS no longer allow holy orders to join.
- The WotRS no longer allow mercs to join unless eligible for a Japanese government.

# Positions:
- The city, wonder, and unit positions for Ningdu should now actually be in Ningdu.
- Units in Lijiang are now, you know, actually in Lijiang.

# Scripted triggers
- can_have_japanese_monastic_feudal_trigger now works, as opposed to potentially evaluating to "yes" when it shouldn't.
- Mourning should now be available when expected.
- It is no longer possible to appoint landed characters as councillors while they are in seclusion, seeing as it would force them and their court out of seclusion. That feels like a Tzeentchian move, and he does not approve of spreading Grandfather Nurgle's love.
- Peasant Leaders are no longer permitted to join the WotRS; they are lowly people, even compared to merchants!
- Set up nickname "coolness" for various new nicknames.

# Scripted effects
- The MoH rating now suffers more from the presence of the Black Death than the presence of some lesser disease.
- Regular MoH updates now require passing certain realm size thresholds to reach Average or higher ratings if not already at the rating in question or at a higher rating.
- Fixed a mourning target bug.
- Various JD Chinese character spawns should now work.
- Improved on the MoH calculation, making it scale better with realm health and simultaneously fixing some issues where it might go very crazy.
- Raw realm size now matters less for the MoH calculation, seeing as there now are realm size thresholds for increasing the rating. Should hopefully help smaller (but not tiny) realms a bit.
- Very small CI realms have now been told to just die already (read: Will get severe penalties to their MoH rating due to their realm size).
- Fixes to some parts of the JD character creation that needs a character scope to work.
- The LNY year flag updates now always fire when someone starts preparing a LNY, as opposed to there possibly being a lack of updates.
- The LNY element calculation now checks the correct variable everywhere, as opposed to going off of the character's birth year everywhere.

# Council voting
- Special considerations towards the Tenno, the Ryukyuan knockoff, and various PRs and the dynasty members thereof should no longer come into play in irrelevant places.

# Minor titles
- Remover duplicate revoke_trigger block for the GC minor title.

# On actions
- Courtiers that get a new host for reasons other than imprisonment will now join their new host in seclusion if their new host is in seclusion. Fixes an issue where e.g. new spouses might get diseases because they married someone in seclusion and got stuck wandering a diseased province... but of course means that diseased courtiers joining someone in seclusion might cause interesting things to happen.

# Execution
- Adjusted the availability of a few options.

# Events
- Chinese Princesses that are abroad should now lose the marriage blocker if abroad on the update (e.g. on_yearly_pulse for the EoC), meaning that inviting them to your court as a foreigner actually might be beneficial.
- Members of the Desecrators and the Mandate Defiers can now get missions to desecrate temples belonging to holders that are considered "Close enough", not just holders of their own religion.
- Rulers that have no reason to care should no longer be informed about the start of the Viking AGe.
- The War of Honor mission should no longer be handed out against fellow vassals if any liege has prevented vassal wars.
- Amaterasu and Amamikiyu now prevent Secret Bears from spawning in the Tenno's family or the knockoff's family.
- Fellow members of non-secret societies will no longer get "A court chaplain accused a member of my society of being a SRS/DW member; I should keep a low profile!" event.
- Wives/concubines lying about their childrens' fathers now also need to beat the Intrigue skill of their husband/primary party, not just have a nifty trait.
- Pilgrimages to Ise and Kumano no longer send you to the opposite location as a Shinto character.
- Decadence Revolters can no longer attack a target that's the primary defender in a Crusade/Jihad/GHW; it can invalidate the relevant war, which is no fun for anyone.
- Your female relatives should no longer tell you that they'd make great councillors when they are imprisoned, incapable, and/or inaccessible.
- Demon Hunters no longer go after inaccessible, incapable, and/or imprisoned DWs; they're either neutralised or hard to target at the moment.
- Excuses not to mourn should be proper.
- The WotRS should now be a bit faster at tracking down the Six, and the Unknown Master should also show up a bit more rapidly.
- Imprisoned, incapable, and/or inaccessible lieges should no longer be asked for nicknames.
- Imprisoned, incapable, and/or inaccessible courtiers should no longer be asking for nicknames.
- It is no longer possible to blame the Jews for epidemics for most rulers that would be ineligible to expel them with the normal decision, thus making it far less likely that random characters unlikely to have a Jewish presence in their realm will see fit to expel them when there is an epidemic.
- The farrier's apprentice/carpenter's daughter event should no longer hand out the Homosexual trait when the other child is of the opposite sex.
- The attractive peasant/courtier event now hands out orientation traits correctly, as opposed to going "You're the same sex as yourself, so the attractive person must have the same sex!" and handing out the Homosexual trait accordingly.
- Mainland SEAsia should no longer notify the world about the Black Death twice for the same epidemic.
- The WotRS will now only tell you to duel your rivals to the death if you're both part of the WotRS.
- Event 4055 ("Peasants saw a dragon!") event can no longer fire in China/Japan/etc., seeing as they shouldn't try to hunt down dragons there (Fredak, the Emperor of Embers, very much excepted).
- You can no longer steal your rival's skull if they are not part of the same top realm on death.
- Widows and children that are imprisonmed, inaccessible, and/or incapable will no longer catch you stealing skulls.
- Various RIP events now sanity check for imprisonment, incapacity, and inaccessibility, as opposed to assuming that just because someone was available when a character died they will keep on being available.
- The pyramid constructed to house the remains of a hunting dog now only lasts for 25 years, as opposed to lasting forever.
- Japanese Feudal characters and members of the WotRS will no longer write "The Sword and Buckler Manual"; shields are for cowards!
- MNM.10024 now only fires if the character actually is taking an interest in a society, as opposed to suggesting that you should refound the " " society if you stop showing interest in a society.
- The stacking negative modifier for unreasonable postponed mourning should now stack properly.
- Courtiers that are expected to mourn should now, you know, mourn.
- Disabled War focus spear-throwing contests for now; will need replacement, re-flavouring, or other adjustments, because not everyone would think throwing spears is reasonable for a warrior.
- Ranking up in the WotRS now clears the quest to find a mentor, seeing as only Novices can get mentors.
- Titles granted by the Tenno will now default to Agnatic or Agnatic-Cognatic most of the time.
- WotRS members should no longer try to discredit *random* society members in some events; they should try to discredit fellow WotRS members.
- WotRS poem events should now attempt to select at least one valid attraction target.
- People that have no reason to care no longer get notified of saints' tombs attracting pilgrims.
- WoL.11204 now properly notifies you about trait loss/gain.
- The Chuseok event chain can no longer convert people to the Reformed Muist religion ahead of the Muist Reformation.
- Hopefully fixed all erraneous "noreligion" conversion tooltips.
- Pilgrim physicians now get the correct pilgrim trait.
- The Gates of Hell now only open for (publicly) Christian characters.
- You will no longer get opportunities to buy horses for your spouse while inaccessible.
- Event 66110 should no longer involve your relatives asking for suicidal wars, at least not prior to allies being considered.
- Event 66110 now properly checks for China forbidding wars everywhere relevant.
- DWs now pick a saner province to mess with the peasants in, as opposed to picking their location regardless of whether their location is in foreign lands, a sea province, or otherwise unsuitable.
- AI characters set to never convert should no longer convert to secret religions through recruitment or randomly.
- Holy order grandmasters and rel heads should no longer be interested in secret religions.
- Zealous AI rulers should no longer sometimes go "Well, *maybe* I should pick up a secret religion...?".
- DW and SRS society leaders can now get missions, bringing those society types in line with other society types.
- Improved conditions for event 30004.
- "The Messenger" should now be someone that would consider ROOT compatible, not just someone ROOT considers compatible.
- Various DWs no longer outsource the very prestigious role of "The Messenger" to members of random societies; the relevant event now restricts the possible existing member to a society member based on the flag ROOT has, rather than ruling out specific DW societies (but not *other* societies) if ROOT doesn't have a matching flag.
- Increased AI Hwarang joining willingness a bit.
- WotRS mentor-student opinion modifiers are now properly cleared up when leaving the society, and the other party remembers it.
- Hwarang Spiritual Guidance opinion modifiers now get cleared out when leaving the Hwarang.
- The WotRS will no longer tell you to go after distant landlocked targets just because you have a coastal province; the target also needs a coastal province now.
- The Tenno and the Ryukyuan knockoff should no longer lose their special succession laws on a reload.
- Characters that have no ancestors to mourn no longer suffer for not mourning.
- Characters that are actively mourning no longer suffer for "not mourning".
- Tweaks to extra child chance logic.
- WotRS mentors and students will now be informed if their student/mentor leaves the WotRS or dies.
- WotRS members will now be informed about the death of a Hatamoto.
- Added an event to help the AI get WotRS mentors, which will particularly help unlanded Novices.
- Seduction should no longer result in a scandal if the seducer and the seducee enter into a marriage or a consort relationship between when the seduction happens and when it is discovered by someone.
- The Hanami sparring session no longer has a bunch of (seemingly) empty random list options.

- A new Saio/Saiin now loses all government traits, to ensure nothing strange happens.
- The Saio/Saiin will now be referred to as such.
- Breakaway pretender emperors should now get a proper starting MoH setup.
- Divine Ancestry rel head titles will now be destroyed on_death if the current heir is not part of the relevant bloodline, assuming the current holder is part of the relevant bloodline.

- Made random deaths slightly rarer based on close relatives' real tier.
- Swaying and Antagonizing will no longer progress if you are inaccessible, incapable, or imprisoned.
- Swaying and Antagonizing will no longer progress if your target is inaccessible, incapable, or imprisoned.
- Ancestor Worship lovers now need a compatible orientation, and cannot be Celibate.
- Various Hunting Focus events now properly announce trait gain.
- Your nemesis now needs a higher intrigue than the victim when tricking you during the "Songs of Anguish" DW event chain.
- RIP hospital events no longer fire in inappropriate circumstances, e.g. while inaccessible.
- Non-theocratic religious authorities no longer demand hospital funds, because that's possibly silly.
- RIP prosperity modifier events no longer fire in inappropriate circumstances, e.g. while inaccessible.

- Councillors should no longer approach secluded/etc. lieges -- or approach lieges while secluded/etc. -- with their nifty ideas.
- Court Chaplains can no longer convert characters that are flagged to never convert or that are inaccessible, incapable, or imprisoned.
- Court Chaplains should no longer suspect same secret religion characters of heresy, excepting rivals.
- Court Chaplains can no longer convert pagan courtiers or vassals while those are inaccessible, incapable, or in seclusion.
- Court Chaplains can no longer squabble if either party is in seclusion or if the province's owner is inaccessible, incapable, or imprisoned.
- Other Court Chaplain sanity fixes.
- Added some sanity checks for some Chancellor events.
- Non-tribal non-nomadic Ancestor Worshippers now get to offer a small amount of incense instead of a carved idols, because carved idols are not suitable in some places.
- You should no longer go "My concubine is pregnant? Great!" and then immediately go "My concubine is pregnant, but I was away, wasn't I?" when it comes to the same concubine and pregnancy.
- WoL.1033 no longer cause the spouse you attack in WoL.1012 to attack themselves. Funny, but no.
- Demonic blessings in various DW events can now only be granted to the Satanists, seeing as the demons involved are weird for everyone else.
- DW Grand Rituals are now only available for the Satanists, seeing as the demons involved are weird for everyone else.
- Courtiers should only attempt to spread "vile heresies" if their religion indeed could be considered a "vile heresy" (or a "vile parent religion") of the target's religion or the religion group is either Christian or Muslim.
- Courtiers flagged to never convert will not embrace vile heresies or accept reconversion, debated or not.
- Characters with secret religions will no longer publicly (and falsely) convert to local religions "just because".
- Characters with secret religions will no longer attempt to spread "vile heresies" (they will, however, still try to spread their secret religions, no matter how vile, heretical, or both).
- Characters with secret religions will no longer listen to courtiers' attempts to spread "vile heresies".
- Chinese/Japanese government rulers are no longer forced to listen to those that would spread "vile heresies" when unable to debate them.
- Various Tianxia feasts no longer hand out non-childhood traits to non-adults, excepting a couple of special cases.

- Children no longer get approached by burglar acrobats at the LNY.
- Children no longer get to bet on lion dancers.
- Somewhat improved the event picture for the random Chinese/Japanese province religion conversion events.
- The option to sacrifice your own child to avoid being killed by a Demon Hunter no longer shows a bunch of random "X is killed" options.
- WotRS mentors (and students) should now be better at correctly recognizing foreign religions.
- The prestige rewards for looking down on -- or not looking down on -- shirabyoshi are now more sensible.
- Improved portrait in WotRS poetry events.
- Non-rulers should no longer have the option to somehow get "their" Court Physician new books/tools.
- Player Court Physicians will no longer be told that they've requested new books/tools; being told that you've done something you've not done isn't good for player choice.
- Characters flagged to never convert in AI hands will no longer randomly flip between Chinese/Japanese religions.
- Characters with non-Chinese/Japanese secret religions and publicly Chinese/Japanese religions will no longer bother to randomly change their public religion.
- The AI will not randomly convert to the secret religion of a rival spouse/consort if that's the only random source of said conversion.
- If you are the GC for the EoC and you are asked about Tribute/Boon acceptance, the event picture will now help indicate whether you should approve or not.
- If you are the EoC and are approached about a Tribute/Boon, the event picture will now help indicate whether everyone is on board/neutral or not.
- Added tooltips telling the EoC and the GC whether they'd be subverting expectations when it comes to Grace events.
- The randomly selected Chinese princess for an Imperial Marriage is now more likely to be an existing lover of her would-be husband if that's an option, though not being a lover of the EoC still takes precedence.
- MO pilgrimage quest completion should now show the proper portrait, as opposed to showing a possibly unrelated portrait.
- Manichaean merchant Court Physicians are now Uyghur rather than Khitan, seeing as the latter makes a lot less sense.
- Chinese Court Physicians created when using the "Recruit Court Physician" decision (or when the relevant event chain is triggered from elsewhere) now actually spawn, as opposed to the event not creating a character and speaking of a village drunkard.
- Viceroys now pass on all artefacts to their player heir rather than their primary heir, excepting any artefacts with special inheritance rules (e.g. the Imperial Regalia of Japan).
- MoH rebel reinforcements no longer attempt to join an unrelated nationalist revolt.
- Adjusted the event borders for some events to make them a bit more fitting.
- Added event borders for some events that lacked them (and therefore used the default).
- Holy order founding events now show the correct character portraits.
- The "Seduce guard to escape from prison" event now requires the Master Seductress trait rather than the Seduction Focus, seeing as the Seduction Focus has been disabled for prisoners.
- Attempted fix of revolters struggling together and consequently frequently hanging separately.
- Minor tweaks to event pictures.
- The AI is now less likely to find the Great Dharani Sutra.
- Pacifist AI characters are no longer particularly interested in a pirate's life.
- Made sure children cannot be targeted by the "flirting at confession" event, because apparently vanilla forgot that check.
- Disabled HL merc sympathy/conversion events and HL merc seduction/duel events for now; mercenary_employer is apparently the merc, which is a bit of a problem...
- Fixed some adultery weirdness potentially arising from event 20, particularly when there is no event target character around.
- Added notification events for JD characters recruited by decision.

# Localisation
- The mod is now less "literally unplayable".
- Nepali culture now uses more standard Indo-Aryan ruler title names for king- and emperor-tier rulers; fixes possible confusion about "Raja"s with the wrong tier.
- The Taiwanese culture group is now properly capitalised.
- MoH updates should no longer refer to the wrong character in the narrative event.
- The Desecrators and would-be members thereof will no longer speak of "black shaman[s]".
- Hanami events no longer speak of "mu vassals".
- soh.2764 option B is now properly capitalised.
- The EXPLANATION entries for various opinion_of_<trait> and <trait>_opinion entries now start with the proper amount of spaces.
- tianxia.906's loc is now properly considered English, as opposed to being improperly considered French.
- Bodyguard lovers are no longer made into "squires" when promoted to courtier status, seeing as squires are not a thing in many places.
- MNM.4441 no longer has weird loc for opposite sex event targets.
- The loc should no longer be broken when arriving at the Chrysanthemum Throne during a Shinto pilgrimage.
- Added formerly missing comma in EVTDSC_RIP_29601_B.
- Reduced use of "\n" in Tianxia loc in order to reduce the risk of loc overflowing things.
- A female Taoist Court Chaplain is now referred to as a "Libationer" rather than a "Upadhyayani".
- Sway events should no longer speak of "hymns"; those aren't a thing everywhere, vanilla!
- WotRS faith poems now mention the faith, as opposed to having " " in the loc.
- The WotRS society interface no longer speaks of "Lodge Commanders".
- Fixed issue with WoL.11203's loc.
- soh.4102 option A now has better loc.
- Various Black Death notifications no longer reference the wrong High Gods.
- Fixed missing quotation mark in the desc for event 100203.
- The loc for 100231 has been updated, getting rid of "church" and incorrect pluralisation of Japanese titles.
- Lunatics no longer confuse Glitterhoof with themselves when considering armour for the former; they're not *that* crazy!
- DWs should now only use the Tears of Demeter and the Blood of Jahi if they have reason to call their drugs of choice either.
- DW orgies no longer speak of "The Great Serpent".
- DW grandmasters no longer speak of serving "The True Lord".
- The tooltips in event 61216 are now less weird.
- RoI.180 now tells you where the kung fu monks show up, as opposed to speaking of a non-specific "the province".
- Fixed "Shinto" in FotK currency gain.
- The FotK and the Bön MO no longer speak of "Dharmic Wisdom".
- ZE.12110 no longer speaks of "alms", and potentially does not speak of a "temple" either.
- Event 1004 option B no longer speaks of "Heaven", seeing as it can fire anywhere.
- Oriya kings are now titled Gajapati, Oriya queens Gajapatni.
- Fixed some issues related to the loc regarding JD Chinese characters spawning due to a bloodline.
- RIP.11550 no longer speaks of pickled boar's heads; that's not something that makes sense in quite a few places.
- Added some missing event loc for new WotRS events.
- Execution and Banishment votes no longer have broken loc.
- There should no longer be angry red text when publicly adopting a safe secret religion by decision.
- The History of the Northern Dynasties now has the proper name.
- Fixed several issues with Ancestor Worship loc.
- Fixed an issue with Hanami poetry contest loc.
- Fixed "the wisdom of [blank]" in various MO events for FotK (and Bön MO) members.
- Fixed an issue where new EoCs/pretenders didn't know the name of their own dynasty.
- Women should now seek to become exalted among women.
- Most minor titles now use "Mistress" instead of "Master" when held by women.
- There's now an "a" in the "Becaome a Japanese Bureaucracy" decision.
- has_dharmic_or_reformed_trigger corrected to has_dharmic_religion_trigger.
- Certain DW events should no longer speak of e.g. "My brother and brother in darkness".
- Spymasters no longer get murdered as a consequence of discovering a plot in "All realm provinces".
- DW events no longer explicitly reference Hell, seeing as that's possibly very weird in places.
- SR sympathy evokers no longer unnecessarily capitalize "Pagan".
- Event 75004 no longer speaks of "the Church" or "the [Religion] Church", seeing as it isn't restricted to Christian characters and as e.g. "the Sunni Church" is nonsensical.
- Fixed "Root.GetSpymasterName]" (lacking an opening square bracket) in a tooltip.
- Fixed two instances where the Marshal council position wasn't properly localised in an event.
- You should now curse the infidels less if you don't particularly care about the outcome of a crusade, e.g. when the defender wasn't from your religion group.
- Characters with a trait that's got "pilgrimage = yes" now have slightly different explanation for what they might be doing, seeing as that shows up on non-pilgrimages too (e.g. Permanent Regencies and TF voyages).
- Unfaithful spouses should hopefully no longer confuse themselves with the spouse they're cheating on.
- Characters should hopefully no longer confuse their lovers' spouses with their lovers.
- Swaying events (and possibly other events) should no longer speak of "the landscape of <capital>, and particularly around <capital>".
- Improved some loc strings to no longer read e.g. "fellow Taoist Eastern"; they will now read "fellow <religion>".
- Hanami shirabyoshi are now referred to with their full names rather than their "best names", avoiding unnecessary mentions of their "the Shirabyoshi" nickname.
- You should no longer be told of the Bones of the First Pope when receiving a JD or Tianxia T5 artefact from an artefact hunt.
- "Magnificent" is now properly spelled, fixing several instances of the mod being "literally unplayable".
- Silk Road religion spread now has the correct religion in the loc.
- Other minor loc tweaks.



Beta 14.0.0 - 09/12/2023
--------------------------------------

IMPORTANT: Tianxia has now been rebased off of CleanSlate; CleanSlate 1.0 Beta 6.1 [EZKM] (https://github.com/ck2plus/CleanSlate/releases/tag/1.0-beta6.1) MUST be active for Tianxia to run.
IMPORTANT: Any old Tianxia.mod file MUST be replaced with the new one; the new version contains important changes, such as actually setting up CleanSlate as a dependency.
IMPORTANT: Old saves are expected to be horribly broken; you have been warned.
IMPORTANT: Random/Shattered world and the EU4 Converter are not supported.
IMPORTANT: Start dates after 1127.4.20 remain unsupported; it's possible to start in them, but they're not expected to be in a good spot, and are not a priority to fix.

NOTE: This version has been in development for a good amount time, with several things being iterated on more than once. As a consequence, some things might be missing below and others might be incorrect.

# General
- Optimized various things.
- Only feudal (is_feudal = yes) characters need apply to be Permanent Regents, Grand Chancellors, or Shoguns.
- Removed stored_mandate_change; the MoH variable is now affected more directly.
- Rulers who lack religious branch traits due to vanilla oversight should now gain branch traits automatically by event after some time.
- Mourning celibacy and regular celibacy should now be cleared in some cases if someone isn't celibate, say because they're tumbling a seducer.
- Added sanity checking for some trait/bloodline inheritance stuff where bastardry comes into play.
- Reminded the Tenno that he does not serve; he rules!
- Wrapped many random_lists without visible effects in hidden_tooltip. Some of them might be replaced with visible custom_tooltips later.
- Created credits.txt, to both ensure that credits can be found outside the forum thread and to facilitate easier updating thereof.

# Ambitions
- Made it easier to start the ambition to become a Grand Chancellor. Now requires 2000 prestige or your liege having a regency (was 5000 prestige).
- The "Become Grand Chancellor" ambition is now available even if your liege is from your dynasty.
- The "Become Grand Chancellor" ambition is now available even if a dynasty member of yours is on your liege's council with such a bloodline.
- The "Become Grand Chancellor" ambition is now available even if your liege has a fully depowered council.
- Confucians and members of a religion with the A Focus on Family Doctrine are now more likely to pick the Family Focus.
- Confucians and members of a religion with the A Focus on Family Doctrine are now more likely to pick the Groom an Heir Ambition.
- Confucians and members of a religion with the A Focus on Family Doctrine now gain 100 piety when completing the Groom an Heir Ambition.
- Taoists now have access to the "Reject [Negative Trait]" ambitions.
- The ambition to become the Grand Chancellor now requires JD + Conclave.
- Characters with a bureaucratic government now only need to wait one year (as opposed to five) to take the "See the Realm Prosper" ambition if it is cancelled, and only five years (as opposed to 25) if it succeeds.
- Added a "Become a famous writer" ambition, requiring you to write books with a combined artefact level of 10+.
- Blocked "Become King of X" ambition when that's impossible, e.g. under a CI liege that doesn't allow vassal kingdom creation.
- Considering a certain job requirement, becoming a Council Eunuch no longer counts for the "Become Councillor" ambition (players can't be Council Eunuchs anyway; landed characters are ineligible).
- Fixed issues with ambition selection for women under equal laws or doctrines.
- Female Enatic rulers will now plot to kill their husbands if they don't have a daughter and their husband is an eunuch.
- Added a new ambition for Taoists to become Immortal. This makes it more likely that the "Matters of Life and Death" event will fire, and will do other things down the line.
- obj_marry_ruler now respects gender equality.
- Homosexual characters no longer aspire to marry rulers.
- Replaced the free upgrades from obj_create_wonder with a bloodline; said upgrades are NOT valid choices for all wonders in Tianxia, and getting an unwanted upgrade as a "reward" does not feel very nice.
- Completing obj_create_wonder now gives the "the Architect" nickname if you don't already have a "super cool" nickname.
- Characters that oppose getting married will no longer take ambitions to get married or have children. Yes, marriage isn't a strict requirement, but it's still odd.

# Artefacts and the spawning thereof
- Changed the Sarira to spawn for has_dharmic_religion_trigger = yes characters, rather than indian_group characters, which should make it less likely to show up in weird places.
- The Heirloom Seal of the Realm is no longer guaranteed to spawn after 936; rediscovery TODO.
- The Seven-Branched Sword now spawns for the character owning c_yamato rather than the Tenno, seeing as it is stored in Isonokami Shrine in Nara Prefecture (and also isn't part of the Imperial Regalia).
- Sugari no Ontachi now spawns for the character owning c_ise rather than the Tenno, seeing as it is stored at Ise Grand Shrine (and also isn't part of the Imperial Regalia).
- Simplified the logic for the Tenno's artefact spawns.
- Fixed issue where extra copies of The Tale of Genji spawned instead of The Pillow Book. Sei Shonagon was not amused.
- Renamed the Murasaki Shikibu Diary Emaki to the Murasaki Shikibu Nikki Emaki.
- The Murasaki Shikibu Nikki Emaki is now more likely to spawn for women.
- The Murasaki Shikibu Nikki Emaki no longer is more likely to spawn for Minamotos.
- Cura Si Manjakini should now spawn for the holder of c_temasek in 1299 or later regardless of their primary title.
- Changed the Sarira to only be usable by Buddhists rather than all Eastern (indian_group) characters, since that's more accurate.
- Fixed incorrect "buddjist" flag on the Sarira.
- Changed the Tooth of Buddha to be Buddhist rather than indian_group.
- Lowered Kusanagi's Japanese/Yamato opinion boost to +2.
- Lowered Yata no Kagami's Japanese/Yamato opinion boost to +2.
- Lowered Yasakani no Magatama's Japanese/Yamato opinion boost to +2.
- Lowered Sugari no Ontachi's Japanese/Yamato opinion boost to +2.
- Sugari no Ontachi is no longer incorrectly flagged as part of the Imperial Regalia of Japan, and can once more be stored in Great Works (if you want to).
- Ryukyuan and Reformed (non-Dogmatic) Ryukyuan characters can now use and be gifted the Totsuka-no-Tsurugi.
- Ganjiang and Moye can now be used regardless of culture and religion.
- The Seven-Branched Sword is now usable by everyone that's not the Tenno (he should of course use the Imperial Regalia instead).
- Kogarasu Maru no longer gives Shinto opinion.
- Dojigiri no longer gives Shinto opinion.
- Onimaru no longer gives Shinto opinion.
- Mikazuki no longer gives Shinto opinion.
- Odenta no longer gives Shinto opinion.
- Lowered Juzumaru's Buddhist opinion boost to +5.
- Juzumaru no longer gives Shinto opinion.
- The Dragon-Phoenix Blade now gives vassal opinion rather than general opinion.
- Lowered the Chinese opinion boost given by the Dragon-Phoenix Blade to +5.
- The Heirloom Seal of the Realm no longer gives vassal opinion.
- Various religious opinion boosts from the Heirloom Seal of the Realm are now +5.
- The King of Na Gold Seal's Chinese opinion boost is now +5.
- Lowered the Cura Si Manjakini's vassal opinion boost to +5.
- Hellenic and Reformed Hellenic characters can now use the Cura Si Manjakini (if Malay, Greek, or member of an Alexander bloodline).
- Sang Sapurba's bloodline now makes you eligible to use Cura Si Manjakini.
- Lowered Vijaya's Hindu opinion boost to +5.
- Gram now gives +1 combat rating.
- The Ornate Holy Symbol artefact can now be gifted to someone with the same religion rather than true_religion, as it otherwise reveals someone's secret religion.
- Lowered the PCS given by most Tianxia weapon artefacts; Kusanagi, Vijaya, Thuan Thien, the Tenka-Goken, and the Hungering Blade are now +20, rest are +16 or less.
- Assorted Korean book artefacts now actually check for e.g. Koreanic + Buddhist/Taoist/Confucian rather than Koreanic OR Buddhist/Taoist/Confucian.
- The Crown of Thorns now is in the grubby hands of the Latin Empire rather than completely missing following the Fourth Crusade (prior to being transferred to France in 1238).
- Nerfed the Nihon Shoki and the Kojiki and lowered their quality.
- Updated the conditions for various artefacts.
- The Totsuka no Tsuragi now spawn when the game starts, instead of being discoverable.
- Added roughly 80 new Chinese books.
- Set up spawning of most of the above.
- The Classic of Poetry now gives +5 Poet opinion, on top of previous benefits.
- Added a few new Chinese artefacts to be discovered.
- Added a few new non-weapon Japanese artefacts.
- Set up spawning for the above.
- Added Preah Khan Reach, and set it to spawn for the holder of the Khmer Empire from 850 onwards.
- Historical Korean authors no longer write wibbly-wobbly, timey-wimey books.
- Added new smith-created swords for Chinese, Japanese, and Dharmic characters.
- Both incarnations of a certain dragonslayer will now refuse to wear helmets. It's just not his thing!
- Added a new dragonslayer artefact.
- Chinese Calligraphy now increases vassal opinion by +1 instead of town opinion by +2.
- Blocked the ability to rename the Heirloom Seal of the Realm and various pagan reformer artefacts, in addition to the Imperial Regalia of Japan.
- Added the reformer_artifact flag to assorted HF temporal reformation artefacts; makes it possible to check if an artifact is any reformer artefact using has_artifact_flag.
- A bunch more Chinese books, mostly ones related to history, are now considered Grace artefacts.
- Added reformer artefacts.
- Commented out chinese_prev_emperor_sculpture (unused in Tianxia), chinese_book_history (we use historical ones instead), and chinese_journal (unused in Tianxia).
- Art of War pages no longer count as books and no longer increase Martial indefinitely; they can instead be used to temporarily increase Martial.
- The Art of War can now be used to temporarily increase Martial, in addition to its previous effects.
- Some Art of War pages will now spawn at the start.
- Restricted the famous_<body part> artefacts to Christians due to them being pretty odd in some places; might revisit down the line.
- Changed prestige rewards for the above to piety rewards, seeing as the Christian loc is "<Body part> of a Saint".
- Removed infamous_tongue artefact; it's quite odd.
- Lunatics may no longer equip other ceremonial weapons if in possession Sanity's Demise. That would be wrong, don't you think...?
- Removed a couple of artefact spawns; relevant artefacts to be moved to "quest" events.
- Removed indestructibility from some artefacts that didn't need it.
- Removed very_rare artefact flag from a bunch of not-so-rare artefacts.
- Added "Seal" slot for artefacts; currently barely used, but likely something that'll be used more in the future.
- Added the Baopuzi and the Danjing yaoyue.
- Artefact "active" blocks now use numerical operators.

# Bloodlines
- All Chinese Imperial bloodlines (including pretenders) now have a better picture.

- Added Divine Ancestry bloodlines for all reformed pagan religions.
- Various Shogunate bloodlines now give access to great heroes (with HF).
- It is now possible to benefit from Grand Chancellor bloodlines if your liege is from your dynasty, but still not if you are independent.
- Improved the Genghis Khan bloodline, and made it available without HF (Sky Burials and other DLC mechanics remain DLC-locked), seeing as it is relevant to Yuan.
- All Grand Chancellor bloodlines (including historical ones) now also increase the likelihood of characters becoming Ambitious, in addition to their previous effects.
- Hachimantaro's bloodline now increases the likelihood of characters becoming Ambitious, in addition to its previous effects.
- The Genghis Khan bloodline now increases the likelihood of characters becoming Ambitious, in addition to its previous effects.
- Added a new bloodline for the "nuclear option" betrayer, if they become the GC.
- Added a bloodline for Munmu of Silla, similar to that available for Taejo of Goryeo.

- Added many, many new Chinese Imperial bloodline names, primarily inspired by historical Chinese dynasties and historical Chinese states.
- Added bloodlines for a number of historical Grand Chancellors.
- Bloodline effects supposed to trigger for Shinto characters only no longer trigger for Qarmatian characters only.
- Added a bloodline for Ma Yin of (Ma) Chu, unlocking his special succession arrangement.

- Added (intentionally very weak) bloodlines to track the agnates of the four Fujiwara branches (Nanke, Hokke, Shikike, and Kyoke).
- The Dongnyeong and Ssangseong bloodlines now lower Koreanic opinion. Choe Tan and Jo Hwi know what they did!
- Wu Zetian's bloodline now stands out a bit more due to having a different icon.

- Set up an empty bloodline for the Saoshyant regardless of DLC, to help ensure proper propagantion of saoshyant_descendant.

- Added new bloodline for obj_create_wonder, making wonder construction both cheaper and faster.
- Wonder bloodlines now give some Architect opinion.
- The "Mercenary Traditions" modifier gained from HL mercs is now a bloodline instead.

# Buildings and wonders
- The tb_defensive_fortifications_NUMBER buildings in tribal holdings can now be built by various defensive Tianxia pagans.

- Zealous non-Confucians can't use the Temple and Cemetery of Confucius, even if their religion otherwise would make them eligible.

- "Civilized" pagans can now build and use Great Libraries and Grand Universities prior to reforming.
- Japonic Buddhists can no longer begin the construction of a Shinto Grand Shrine, but they can still benefit from such wonders unless Zealous.
- The Temple and Cemetery of Confucius now has fewer pre-built upgrades in order to allow for some degree of customization.

- Added a Grand Taoist Temple Wonder, with upgrades.

- Added Bureaucratic Center buildings for various bureaucratic governments.
- Samurai Dojos now grant Horse Archers and Pikemen, not Heavy Infantry.
- Grand Torii now increase monthly prestige, rather than improving Zealous opinion. Grand Torii might be impressive, but Zealous unbelievers certainly would not approve.

- Removed Shrines and Greater Shrines to Izanami and Izanagi; they were rather hard to balance, and the Shinto Grand Shrine Wonder still has plenty of options.

# CBs
- Fixed some potential cases of sacriligious Chrysanthemum Throne usurpation.
- Fixed some incorrect tooltips regarding what would be obtainable from certain CBs.
- Cleaned up old, irrelevant CBs related to Decadence for Chinese dynasties. That hasn't been relevant since JD...
- Cleaned up old, unused Chinese county conquest CB. Another CB that's not ben relevant for a good while...
- Wars to overthrow the Permanent Regent can now end in a white peace, restoring the regent but not punishing the liege aside from a prestige loss.
