- If the prospective Shogun loses the war to become the Shogun and has selected someone as their would-be Tenno this would-be Tenno will now be regarded as a traitor by the Tenno.
- Added Permanent Regent-related factions for the Ryukyuan knockoff.
- Added new CB for valid Imperial Family members to press de facto claims on the Chrysanthemum Throne.
- Added new CB to press the de facto claims of valid Imperial Family members on the Chrysanthemum Throne.
- Religious Liberation is now also blocked if your top liege would be disallowed due to their government.
- "Chinese enough" and "Japanese enough" pagans that are NOT nomads are no longer eligible for the pagan County Conquest CB; such realms are not meant to be expansionistic in that fashion. The optional JD Border Dispute CB has been made avaliable for these rulers if it is enabled.
- The Holy War CB and its pagan and Buddhist counterparts now show up in the GUI for people that'd be eligible if they were independent but that are blocked by their top liege's government type.
- Caliphal Subjugation is no longer available if your top liege has one of the Chinese or Japanese government types.
- Rulers with various Chinese-style governments now get access to the Chinese Subjugation CB against suitable targets when appropriate.
- Rulers with various Japanese-style governments no longer get the Indian Subjugation CB, instead getting a similar CB against suitable targets when appropriate.
- Reworked the Chinese Imperial Reconquest CB, and made it free for the EoC (similar to how the Roman Imperial Reconquest is free for the RE).
- Reworked the Japanese Reconquest CB, and made it free for the Tenno (similar to how the Roman Imperial Reconquest is free for the RE).
- Removed the Mon Rebellion CB and made the relevant war a Bid for Independence war, with Yamankhan getting k_pegu if he wins and it isn't held by anyone.
- Added the Ryukyuan Subjugation CB.
- Kiyohara Masahira should now get the event he is supposed to get if he wins the Gosannen War.
- Children can no longer create Grand Chancellor bloodlines, seeing as they can't be the Grand Chancellor.
- Rising Sun Subjugations now have a 100 year cooldown, same as most other subjugations.
- Rising Sun Subjugations can no longer target a Ryukyuan Tenno knockoff or any liege thereof.
- Rising Sun Subjugations now invalidate if the target becomes the Tenno, the Ryukyuan knockoff, or any liege of either. If this happens, the cooldown is removed.
- Restricted claim CB use somewhat in some cases concerning Chinese/Japanese/Divine Imperial rulers.
- Fixed broken warlord_consolidation prestige cost.
- The Grand Chancellor no longer needs prestige to use the warlord_consolidation CB.
- The Grand Chancellor can now use the warlord_consolidation CB regardless of their liege's MoH rating, provided the realm permits internal warfare.
- Winning a War of Honor against a rival now results in you imprisoning said rival, regardless of whether you were the attacker or the defender, making it slightly more useful.
- Becoming the Grand Chancellor through war now requires JD + Conclave.
- A potential GC that hasn't previously created a GC bloodline will now do so when enthroning a claimant through a faction war, even if they already have such a bloodline.
- The GC is now further empowered if there's a new GC following a faction war even if they did not create a bloodline.
- Shi Jingtang is now correctly viewed as a traitor if he loses or white peaces his starting war.
- Hopefully fixed some instances of peasant/etc. revolters becoming republics. Jumped-up peasant or not, you should prefer being some flavour of feudal!
- However much of a barbarian you might be due to your inferior government type, you may no longer use "But I'm tribal!" as an excuse to circumvent Chinese/Japanese religious CB rules.
- Winning a Chinese Subjugation war as the attacker against a king+ ruler will now award a super cool nickname if you are young enough.
- Assorted potentially relevant CBs that could grant the "the Conqueror" nickname can now grant the "the Little Conqueror" nickname if the conqueror is young enough.
- Papuan county invaders will no longer get the "the Conqueror" nickname, seeing as it's not all that impressive to conquer a county.
- Added Mandate penalties (and sometimes gains) to various CBs.
- Fixed bug letting CI/JI/DI rulers press others' claims.
- Beating the Takeover faction now moves the vassal influence law two steps in the liege's favour.
- Rulers eligible for Chinese/Japanese governments (if feudal) can no longer wage holding wars against holy orders with leaders that technically would be eligible for the same subset.
- Made it harder to push a woman's claim on an Agnatic Chinese Imperial title.
- Rivals will now wage Wars of Honour more often.
- Friends and lovers will now be much less likely to wage Wars of Honour.
- Adjusted some ai_will_do blocks to account for the See the Realm Prosper ambition.
- Shi Jingtang will now usurp Bianjing, Chang'an, and Luoyang if he wins his starting war (assuming they're part of the same realm), and will if possible move his capital to Bianjing.
- Tyranny wars against CI/EI/ConBu rulers now forces abdication to the attacker rather than a dynasty member if won.
- China will now experience the same consequences from losing tyranny wars as from takeover/non-dynastic claimant faction wars.
- Updated the Jin + Song vs. Liao war to no longer target a non-existent tributary.
- Mandate revolters that win their war now institute Primogeniture in their realm.
- Updated Pagan Subjugation to account for new pagans eligible for CI/etc.
- Pagan, Indian, and Caliphal Subjugation can no longer be used with a Chinese/Japanese-style top liege.
- Told new pagans in China to stop with the county conquests already.
- Fixed issue with peasant revolters/etc. going feudal in tribal lands.
- Fixed issue with MoH revolters not getting Primogeniture when they should.
- Fixed issue with the Shogun not getting Primogeniture as he should.
- Japanese Reconquest wars should now work, as intended. RIP Mutsu.
- If eligible, the Child of Destiny will now create a pretender empire upon gaining their first kingdom.
- Changed Go Yeongchang's war against Liao to be an attempt to take over Liao rather than an independence war; it makes more sense that way.
- MoH revolters now get some piety upon winning their revolt, making it possible for them to wage some Chinese Subjugation wars right away.
- Increased range of Rising Sun Subjugation to within four sea zones; it's less fun if the attacker is blocked due to being in the wrong part of Japan
- Rising Sun Subjugations can no longer be waged against WotRS members
- Should a MoH uprising win, the attacker will now create a pretender empire unless there's another Peasant Leader CI emperor around (having everyone do it is a bit much...).
- Added a requirement that the holder holds at least two de jure counties in the target title for the Chinese, Japanese, and Ryukyuan Subjugation CBs, bringing them in line with the Indian Subjugation CB and making it a bit less likely that the AI will waste it.
- Shi Jingtang's event troops now disband on peace. Should make it less likely Jin goes on a conquest spree right after defeating Tang... and should make it less wise to alter a certain deal...
- Kejawèn rulers now have holy wars along Buddhist lines rather than Hindu lines.
- Updated Permanent Tributary CB and related events to account for CI would-be-suzerains.
- The Manifest Destiny CB is now tied to Seljuk and Timur's bloodlines if HF is active, bringing it more in line with other CBs dependent on lineage.
- The Manifest Destiny CB can no longer be used if eligible for a Chinese- or Japanese- style government or if a vassal of a ruler with such a government.
- MRs that are "Chinese enough", "Japanese enough", or that has such a liege or above no longer have access to their coastal city/county conquest CBs. Merchants are useless members of society, after all, so closing such unintended exploits is just and necessary.
- Rulers that are mourning can no longer declare war.
- Tributaries now correctly abdicate their titles after losing a war for increased tributary tier.
- Tributaries no longer get to break free after losing a war for increased tributary tier. China remembers what it declared the war for, thank you very much.
- Losing a tributary tier war as the tributary now forces you to abdicate to China's preferred heir (excluding the Tenno, who merely abdicates "normally"; it's too risky to let China pick).
- Tossed out vanilla's highly questionable "Characters with a capital in Europe needs Majesty 4 to wage permanent tributary wars" condition; it means e.g. moving the ERE's capital from Constantinople to a random Anatolian county unlocks the CB. Everyone that's not got a bloodline making it easier to get tributaries now needs Majesty 2.
- Updated clean_up_after_leaving_society_effect to account for Tianxia's societies.
- chinese_reconquest is now also accessible for independent Chinese culture group ConBu/EI kings/emperors even if there is a CI China, but they may not normally target CI realms (successful Mandate revolters still can).
- warlord_consolidation now has a one year truce, similar to other subjugation CBs. Should make interesting times more interesting.
- Improved CI dynasty flag cleanup for certain historical wars.
- TF punitive expeditions and TF tributary wars now invalidate on CI dynasty change in China.
- nick_the_lionheart is now rather more restricted.
- Kingdom-tier Tianxia CBs now result in Roman triumphs if the target is the ERE/RE and Christian/Hellenic.

# Combat tactics
- Various Tianxia combat tactics have been revised/replaced.
- All Tianxia WLs have unique combat tactics, as one would expect.

# Council voting
- AI Permanent Regents will no longer be Loyalists unless they are friends or lovers of their liege.
- AI councillors eligible for Chinese Imperial (theoretically; this is to cover (Xi-)Xia), Confucian Bureaucracy, or Japanese Bureaucracy now care about their liege's MoH rating when deciding on their voting pattern.
- Moved Japanese council logic (e.g. "No, I will not support executing the Tenno if you or I logically should care!") to a separate voting pattern and made it take precedence over everything else.
- Moved Council Eunuch logic to a separate file and made it relevant for Pragmatists (beating the normal pragmatist pattern), Glory Hounds (below the normal Glory Hound pattern), and Zealots (before the normal family-based logic).
- Added new bureaucrat voting logic for all bureaucratic government vassals with bureaucratic government lieges (Chinese/Eastern/Japanese/Divine Imperial, Confucian/Japanese Bureaucracy). Largely similar to the eunuch logic when it comes to landing/revoking/wars, and inserted at (effectively) the same priority level, but these make exceptions for their own dynasty members.
- Eunuchs and bureaucrats now care about nepotism as far as the liege's dynasty goes (unless it is also their dynasty).
- Added and expanded patterns for new Chinese-style laws.
- Updated voting pattern selection for new Chinese-style laws.
- Council Eunuchs will now oppose imprisoning fellow Council Eunuchs unless Loyalist/forced by a Favour or the target is a rival.
- Council Eunuchs now correctly oppose further empowering vassals.
- Council Eunuchs and bureaucrats will no longer be in favour of granting land to non-feudal (is_feudal = no) rulers, and will generally be okay with revoking stuff from them.
- Council Eunuchs and bureaucrats are now much less likely to support title grants to female characters.
- Japanese Feudal and Japanese Monastic Feudal characters are now much less likely to support title grants to female characters.

# Cultures
- Deleted unused Buryat and Kashmiri cultures.
- Set Papuan culture as used_for_random = no, seeing as it's offmap until/unless the Treasure Fleet zails east/SRI happens and is unlikely to end up landed even when those things happen.
- Set Papuan culture as seafarer = yes. They'll need all the help they can get.
- Added the Kadazan-Dusun culture.
- Adjusted culture setup and added new cultures in China, SEAsia. Updating of some historical characters and palceholders still TODO.
- Added and improved many namelists
- Split the Malayo-Polynesian culture group.
- Viet culture is now in its own culture group.
- Reorganized non-vanilla culture files a bit.
- Yamato and Wako dukes have been reminded that Japan does not do "Kings".
- Khitans no longer name titles after dynasties; it's the Yelü dynasty, vanilla!
- Updated the colour of some cultures.
- Added seafarer = yes to Tamil culture, because Cholas.
- Added seafarer = yes to Malay, Cham, Javanese culture.
- Added parent = roman to Italian and Sardinian.
- Added parent = french to Outremer.
- Corrected gross oversight as far as cat names goes; the best feline overlord is now included!
- Corrected "Tiomat" to "Tiamat". She was not amused.
- Commented out some dragon names in the wrong gender namelist. I'm reasonably sure both Deathwing and Bahamut are male.
- Added a few more dragon names.
- Removed dukes_called_kings = yes from some cultures that shouldn't have petty kings.
- Expanded the Japanese (and Wako) male namelist somewhat.
- Updated secondary_event_pictures for a number of cultures to have them fallback to a similar culture first.
- Revised the Batak namelist, removing many names that were clan names or otherwise terrible, and adding new ones.
- Linked some more names to ensure regnal numbers work as they should in more cases.
- Updated Malay names and derived names.
- Updated Javanese names and derived names slightly and fixed history.
- Improved Tagalog and Cebuan namelists.
- Revised Papuan namelist.
- Replaced Dayak and Banjar namelists with slightly less wrong placeholder namelists.
- Added a new culture related to one very specific Treasure Fleet scenario.
- Cham is now feminist = yes.
- Tagalog and Cebuan are now feminist = yes.
- "Petrus" (Peter) is now a Roman name; vanilla had skipped it for whatever reason, even though a certain Petrus Sabbatius was quite important (you might know him as Ioustinianos/Justinian the Great).

# Death reasons and death text
- Updated "holy warrior" death text to account for Tianxia pagan "Crusaders".
- Opened up death_execution_boiling to all Cruel non-nomads.
- Added a number of new death reasons, some available during gameplay, some for the history files.

# Decisions
- Fixed society recruitment for some Tianxia DW societies not allowing the recruitment of certain Buddhists.
- Made the AI check some decisions a bit less frequently in the name of performance.
- Adjusted prestige costs for the Become Grand Chancellor decision, making it take CI interest group laws into account.
- The decision to convert culture from Emishi to Ainu no longer requires you to have Yamato culture.
- The AI no longer sees a reason to look for a Mass Conversion if they've got Chinese Imperial/etc.
- The AI will no longer bother with changing government
- You no longer lose prestige when changing to Japanese Feudal using the decision (the WotRS decision still costs society currecy, since that's easier), but there's still a prestige requirement to take the decision.
- You no longer lose piety when changing to Japanese Monastic Feudal using the decision (the FotK decision still costs society currecy, since that's easier), but there's still a piety requirement to take the decision.
- You can now convert to Japanese Feudal or Japanese Monastic Feudal without amassing prestige or piety if your top liege has the desired government and shares your dynasty (and you are eligible for the government). to/from Japanese Feudal from/to Japanese Monastic Feudal.
- Removed the ability for Japanese Monastic Feudal to sponsor Mass Conversions and to send missionaries to foreign lands. The Shinto religion doesn't do that, and nor would a Ryukyuan imitation.
- Chinese Imperial emperors with a sub-Average MoH rating or highly influential vassals can no longer demand that vassals make peace or switch to Gavelkind using the targeted decisions.
- The Tenno and the Shogun can no longer demand that vassals make peace or switch to Gavelkind using the targeted decisions if they have a Permanent Regent.
- The EoC no longer accept ingredients as Tributes. Really, even next to assorted other paltry trinkets a random eyeball or herb is an insult.
- Removed irrelevant decision to restore the Manichean leadership; it's around in all start dates in vanilla since a while back.
- The AI now correctly is more interested in receiving Spiritual Guidance if they have one of the related ambitions, rather than being more interested in receiving it from someone that has one of those ambitions.
- The cost for asking someone for Spiritual Guidance is now lower if you've been given a quest to receive it.
- Fixed incorrect references to a non-existent "hwarng" society.
- Stoic culture conversion decision should now work properly for the Stoics again.
- The WotRS decision to become Japanese Feudal is no longer available for characters that immediately will become ineligible.
- WotRS members looking for a mentor will now be less likely to pick a terrible warrior.
- WotRS members looking for a student will now be less likely to approach a terrible warrior.
- Improved some potential/from_potential blocks for WotRS stuff.
- Added a decision for the Tenno to found Heian-kyo if you start in 769. It's kind of an important city, so you should...
- Removed the now irrelevant decision to meddle/cancel meddling in the Tenno's succession.
- Cut the "Hey, can I be your Permanent Regent?" decisions and the "Hey, could you end this whole Permanent Regency thing, please?" decisions. Use the ultimatums, and fight for it if necessary.
- If you or your liege have a bureaucratic government the "Request land for Son/Daughter" decision now requires a valid adult son/daughter.
- Fixed several issues with the regency powers, added a few more, and did some general polishing.
- Added a targeted decision for the Tenno to give the Taira surname to Imperial Family members, working effectively the same way as the decision to give someone the Minamoto surname. This decision is impossible to use after the Genpei War, seeing as the Tairas lost. The AI will not use it, due to the risk of it stupiding the Imperial Family into extinction.
- Added a player-only (the AI does it using an on_yearly_pulse event) decision to restore an agnate to the Imperial Family.
- If the Tenno uses the special decision to recreate Japan while holding the de jure liege title of a Japanese kingdom (including Ezo and Ryukyu) the title in question will instantly de jure drifct into Japan.
- If the Tenno uses the special decision to recreate Japan while holding a Japanese kingdom (including Ezo and Ryukyu) the title in question will instantly de jure drifct into Japan
- Conclave and Horse Lords dynamic mercs can no longer be created by rulers with a bureaucratic (any flavour) top liege. It'd have been very frowned upon to hire out troops you were effectively borrowing from the central government.
- Added a decision to end a Permanent Regency if there is no landed Permanent Regent in the realm (and you're not currently waging war against them), to deal with some possible edge cases.
- The visibility for the JD decision to become Chinese Imperial is now more appropriate.
- The AI no longer needs a ton of prestige to become Chinese Imperial (it still needs as much as the human player would need).
- Mandate of Heaven revolters now get a discount of becoming Chinese Imperial, similar to CI bloodline members.
- The prestige cost for becoming Chinese Imperial now scales based on whether China is held, whether China's holder is Chinese Imperial or Eastern Imperial (or neither), and the MoH rating of the holder if Chinese Imperial, potentially making it very cheap.
- Added AI logic for becoming Chinese Imperial, checking e.g. traits.
- Various conversion decision (e.g. Shinto <-> Buddhist) are now self-targeted targeted_decisions, consistent with vanilla.
- Removed Tengri Jurchen -> Taoist/Buddhist conversion.
- The non-Grace decisions to hire various JD characters (e.g. Sholar-Bureaucrats) now requires both you and your top liege to have one of the Chinese governments, making it more widely available geographically (it was previously restricted to rulers in the China region or under China) but somewhat more restrictive from a government standpoint.
- The decision for e.g. Chinese Imperial rulers to take their de jure capital is now only allowed for king+ rulers.
- The Deed to the Dragon Throne is now only usable by feudal (is_feudal = yes) characters.
- Only the holder of the Grand Chancellor minor title can demand that others on their liege's council backs them using the GC targeted decision.
- Grand Chancellor bloodline members cannot ask the Grand Chancellor minor title holder to give up their spot on their liege's council. They won't fall for *that* trick again!
- Chinese Imperial Guard regiments might now fall under the control of the eunuchs, requiring their tacit approval to use.
- Losing the Mandate of Heaven now means the Imperial Guard regiments can't be hired until you manage to raise your Mandate rating to at least Poor.
- It is no longer possible to request a swap to the Heritage focus if the child is eligible for a Chinese/Japanese government and would be rendered ineligible with your culture/religion.
- It is no longer possible to request a swap to the Faith focus if the child is Shinto and part of the Imperial Family, nor if the child is Reformed Ryukyuan and part of a Ryukyuan knockoff's imperial family.
- You can longer torture, mutilate, humiliate, or eat the Tenno or the Ryukyuan knockoff, assuming you should care. Reading questionable poetry is, however, still on the table.
- Chinese Imperial rulers can no longer become the Samrat Chakravartin; you are China (or want to be), and anything other than the Mandate of Heaven is irrelevant.
- The Tenno can no longer create custom CM empires, to avoid any possibility of an "Empire of Takamikura" abomination forming.
- Dynamic merc titles now use the creator's capital as their capital, which hopefully should make instances of e.g. an Hungarian merc company being hired by the Tenno vanishingly rare.
- The holder of e_chagatai now gets to hold the Nerge rather than Grand Hunts, consistent with the other Mongol successor states.
- Treated vanilla's decision to lock the creation of Antioch and Cyprus to Catholics as a bug, and enabled it for Fraticelli as well since they have the same crusade mechanics and should be able to get the same crusader states.
- Cleaned up irrelevant code for CI transfer from a post-Fourth Crusade title to the ERE since CI rulers can't restore the ERE.
- It is no longer possible to execute the Tenno or the Ryukyuan knockoff using a brazen bull, dragonfire, or the option to add them to a macabre banquet, assuming you should care.
- It is no longer possible to disband PR/Shogunate-related factions with bloodline powers.
- Japonic Buddhist men that are forced to take monastic vows now join the Fellowship of the Kami rather than the regular Buddhist MO.
- Japonic Buddhist men that are forced to take monastic vows that have a martial education or lifestyle will now go to the Sohei, assumingt they exist.
- Chinese Buddhist men that are forced to take monastic vows that have a martial education or lifestyle will now go to the Shaolin Monks and the Shaolin society.
- It is no longer possilbe to burninate the capital of the Tenno or the Ryukyuan knockoff with extreme prejudice if you are someone that should care.
- Japanese/Divine Imperial rulers and their dynasty members can no longer send concubines to China.
- If HF is active unreformed and non-Dogmatic Reformed Tengri rulers can now use the decision to gain sympathy with other religion groups.
- Taoist, Confucian, Shinto, and most Tianxia pagans now have access to the Ancestor Worship decision inherently.
- Non-Zealous Shinto rulers can now gain Sympathy for Pagans by decision if they have a pagan (any flavour) liege, vassal, or province.
- Confucians have access to the Inspire Meliorism targeted decisions if HF is active.
- Assorted Grand Chancellor decisions now require JD + Conclave.
- Minor improvements to various GC decisions.
- The decision to appoint new Council Eunuchs might now select eligible eunuchs in your court instead of generating a new eunuch.
- The decision to appoint new Council Eunuchs is now more expensive for the EoC, seeing as it's somewhat likely he'll have eunuchs around.
- The Tenno no longer gets to employ goldsmiths, seeing as he already possesses all the signs of imperial power he should need (read: The Imperial Regalia). He can, however, use crown jewels that come into his possession in some other fashion.
- The Grand Chancellor bloodline decision's prestige requirement now scales with the liege's vassal influence law, if relevant.
- AI Confucian and Japanese Bureaucracy rulers are now somewhat more likely to write books.
- Nomads settling should now be a bit less likely to create custom titles, seeing as those are no fun to deal with from a scripting perspective.
- Improved the decisions to get special JD characters, making the conditions a bit better and allowing them for feudal rulers with a Chinese character (any subset) bloodline even if they don't have a Chinese government.
- If the Tenno or the Ryukyuan knockoff ends up as a tribal or nomadic ruler through some convoluted loophole they are no longer allowed to become merchant republics.
- Assorted PR, GC, and Shogun decisions are now only available for feudal (is_feudal = yes) characters.
- Added a scaling (based on how China is doing) realm size check for adopting Chinese Imperial.
- Unreformed Shenists now pay less to adopt Chinese Imperial (similar to reformed Shenists, Taoists, and Confucians).
- Made the AI willing to write books when they already have one if having Confucian Bureaucracy, Japanese Bureaucracy, or the ambition to become a famous writer.
- Added a decision to become Chinese Imperial as a king (resulting in the creation of one of the titular CI empires).
- Fixed some caste-related oddities.
- Japonic Buddhist lieges (or above) of the Ryukyuan knockoff can no longer become the Samrat Chakravartin.
- The decision to enact the plot to fabricate a claim on a Chinese Imperial title's chance of succeeding now depends on the holder's MoH rating.
- Added decision for Spiritual religions to gain stat boosts in exchange for piety.
- It's no longer possible to make the parent/grandparent/etc. of your current heir a Minamoto/Taira, seeing as that'd also disinherit your current heir and that that'd thus be a loophole in the "No disinheriting your current heir" limitation.
- The generic tributary breakaway decision now lets a tributary of China instantly break free regardless of tier, paying the Grace to lower tier one step and not letting China dispute this decision. Of course, China might not be happy...
- The generic tributary breakaway decision is now also available if your suzerain has Lost the Mandate of Heaven.
- Tributaries of China cannot break away with the generic decision if they've got a Chinese Peace Deal.
- If China asks for a border adjustment and the target refuses, all relevant titles are claimed by the EoC.
- The prestige cost of overthrowing the Permanent Regency now scales with the number of Regency Power laws that have been enacted, making it much harder to achieve if a powerful PR temporarily isn't the regent.
- Disinherited characters can no longer be legitimized, to prevent certain silliness.
- Shugendo and Kejawen can now syncretize with pagans.
- Many Tianxia pagans can now syncretize with the Eastern religions.
- Sunda Wiwitan and the Minangkabau religion can now syncretize with Islam.
- Tengri rulers can now pick up multiple sympathy traits.
- Made various syncretism decisions consistent with the HF one as far as being capable adults goes.
- Added decisions for a suitable Viet emperor of Vietnam to instantly de jure drift Guangxi and Guangdong into Vietnam, assuming the liege title of those does not exist. Nam Viet shall rise again!
- It is no longer possible to negotiate with raiding adventurers that are at war, as that can result in some silly things.
- Members of the Fellowship of the Kami no longer need Holy Fury to become Japanese Monastic Feudal with the society decision.
- Replaced the Fellowship of the Kami's culture and religion conversion powers with powers to make the relevant religions like you more.
- The Tenno, the Shogun, and the Ryukyuan knockoff can no longer kowtow to China. They're obviously above that!
- Shinto or suitably reformed Ryukyuan theocracies can now potentially become Japanese Monastic Feudal if count+.
- Possession of the Heirloom Seal of the Realm makes both decisions to become Chinese Imperial significantly easier to take... though bypassing the realm size requirement is perhaps not very smart.
- Added a new decision for sufficiently powerful feudal rulers eligible for Chinese Imperial to claim e_china if they possess the Heirloom Seal of the Realm. If a vassal or below of Chna, this means the EoC will consider you a traitor.
- Cleaned up Khitan settling, seeing as Liao now starts as settled.
- Retired emperors can no longer be given surnames, seeing as that'd mean someone not from the Imperial Family has held the Chrysanthemum Throne.
- Removed the kowtow decisions and events; vanilla's setup is really not good for us, and fixing it now would delay release further. Something kowtow-related will be added at some point in the future; details still TBD.
- Heian-kyo should now become the capital when created by decision.
- Yamashiro should now become Japanese when Heian-kyo is created by decision.
- The Imperial Family should now become Japanese along with the Tenno if the Tenno takes the above decision.
- Shugendo and Koshin characters can now ask their dynasty members to become monks/nuns.
- Added decisions related to Imperial Guard regiments.
- The AI should now account for Imperial Guard regiments when considering whether to issue faction ultimatums.
- It is no longer possible to issue an ultimatum for Primogeniture if your liege has been forced into Chinese Succession.
- Dragon hunts can now be attempted further away from Fredak's lair.
- Added a new artefact decision related to a new artefact potentially discovered at Fredak's lair.
- It is now possible to refound the Luojuzi.
- Eastern Imperial rulers can no longer become the Samrat Chakravartin, bringing them in line with the other Chinese-/Japanese-style governments.
- Shinto Japanese Monastic Feudal characters no longer get to convert between Japanese religions by decision, seeing as that'd result in a government change.
- Limited the ability to convert to Jainism somewhat, seeing as the religion effectively didn't leave India historically.
- Chinese artifact searching is now a bit more widely available, but also a bit more expensive.
- Removed the decisions to create specific Chinese artefacts; it potentially resulted in too many artefacts being around.
- Improved Chinese character decision setup, and made them consistent with Grace/Bloodline character creation.
- It is no longer possible to support a Rising Sun Subjugation when none is being prepared.
- Relevant faction ultimatums have been disabled if the "nuclear option" for dealing with eunuchs/courtiers is currently in effect. This avoids some potential issues.
- Cosmopolitan and Tolerant religions now get to pick multiple Sympathy traits if Syncretic.
- Japanese Feudal characters can now go on Grand Hunts; to be replaced down the line.
- No more stealing Yamashiro from the Tenno with PR/Shogun powers.
- Characters that manage to become the Grand Chancellor in any fashion that forges a GC bloodline now get the special_marshal flag.
- Except for the Lord Spiritual position (which goes off of priestly gender) the Grand Chancellor does not care about gender restrictions for their council position.
- The Tenno and the Shogun can now counter meddling from each other and each other's PR.
- The Tenno's PR and the Shogun's PR can now meddle a bit with each other.
- Invited commanders are now Japanese Feudal if inviter is Japanese Feudal.
- Added a decision for Yamato/Japanese Pirates/etc. living on the coast to culture swap to Wako.
- Should the Tenno somehow lose Yamashiro to the Shogun he can now retake it by decision.
- Closed loophole where holy orders could get baronies in unwanted places due to building hospitals.
- Members of a Grand Chancellor bloodline can now ask for a spot on their liege's council from their liege in addition to being able to ask a councillor to step aside for them. This should make it a bit more likely that they will end up in a position of power.
- Kejawén rulers can now also change their caste traits.
- No dissolving factions if the Mandate of Heaven has been Lost.
- Monastic Orders with access to province religious conversion decisions no longer get to convert heretic strongholds; likely vanilla oversight, seeing as the modifier would remain in the province.
- Monastic Order religious conversion of provinces now counts for the Strengthen Religion ambition.
- You, too, can now be known for hating the sunlight, should you be a Greek that's overly fond of blinding your prisoners.
- Removed Jurchen troops on special CI empire creation; they're already having an easier time than others.
- Donations, joining, expulsion of holy orders set up.
- The Myrmidons and the Children of Dralha now get the usual pagan HO events and decisions if SoA is installed; likely vanilla oversight.
- Added a knockoff "Create India" decision for Muslims, given their historical attempts to conquer the subcontinent. Requires RoI.
- Added founding decisions for Tianxia's non-Buddhist Eastern HOs.
- Nomads, courtiers in nomadic courts, and characters following a Cosmopolitan or Tolerant religion will no longer go "You can't seduce me; we belong to different religion groups!". Note that the AI doesn't seduce anyone outside its own realm, so this will not make it go even crazier.
- "Japanese enough" and "Chinese enough" characters can now seduce foreigners across religion group borders inside their own "group". Again, it'll not make the AI seduce more people.
- The AI will now check "Do I have *a* compatible spouse/consort"? in seduction logic, as opposed to "Do I have *any incompatible* spouse/consort?", making it a bit less eager to seduce (though Lustful characters are not restricted by this...). Vanilla stupidity.
- AI women will no longer consider a lack of a spouse or consort cause to NOT seduce someone when Homosexual; the risk of having a bastard isn't a factor. Vanilla stupidity.
- Updated tributary breakaway decision to account for the EoC.
- Improved AI Rising Sun Invasion decision logic.
- Various conversion decisions (e.g. "Convert to attacker's religion") can now be taken by Zealous characters if their secret religion matches the religion they're converting to, seeing as that's pretty reasonable. Vanilla oddity (plus consistency improvements).
- The AI now considers moving China's capital on_decade_pulse if from a relevant dynasty, as opposed to checking a decision rather more broadly.
- The Living Flame is now one use only.
- All holy sites now have Protection from Evil (and Protection from Chaos), rendering them impossible to target by the Living Flame.
- Added Pagan GHW unlock decision and announcement event.
- Founding or refounding an Imperial Guard regiment now gives you a small MoH boost.
- Reclaiming an Imperial Guard regiment now gives you a small MoH boost.
- Fixed incorrect prestige penalties for the GC's preferences when it comes to Tributes/Boons.
- Added decision to pay money to strengthen your religion, seeing as not everyone has access to holy wars or religious liberation wars.
- Opened up hunts for women under more circumstances; now Enatic Clans reformers, non-Agnatic Clans Minahasan and Minangkabau rulers, WL members, and rank 2+ WotRS members can also hunt.
- Imperial Guard regiments can no longer be founded, refounded, raised, or reclaimed in the middle of the "nuclear option".
- Added missing Hellenic secret conversion decision on holy sites.
- The Ainu WL power now also checks the society rank of the would-be concubine/consort if they are in the Ainu WL, meaning higher rank ones will refuse offers from people that either don't have a high ruler tier or a high society tier.
- Opened up LNY eligibility slightly.

# Defines
- Increased the event offsets to 30 (rulers) and 75 (courtiers); helps a bit with performance.

# Disease:
- Increased the outbreak chance of various diseases by 50 %, seeing as we've got roughly 50 % more land provinces than vanilla. Might need further tweaking.
- Updated several patterns to account for geographical region changes not previously accounted for.
- Unless set to "Historical" the Black Death can now spawn in various new and exciting places, spreading Grandfather Nurgle's love more evenly.

# Dynasties
- Added new dynasties for many cultures.
- The notorious "Smith" dynasty should now be much rarer.
- Taiwanese is no longer used as a placeholder for Tenggaran.
- Achenese is no longer used as a placeholder for Malukan.
- Added a bunch of Kadazan-Dusun dynasty names.
- The Yelü dynasty is now properly named as such.
- The "Kara-khitai dynasty" is no longer a thing. Still note lack of honour!
- Adjusted name and/or culture of some vanilla Khitan dynasties that looked very suspect.
- Changed CoAs for Buddhist dynasties with pre-scripted CoAs to still use the Hindu set.
- Changed CoAs for some pagans with pre-scripted CoAs to still use the Norse set.
- Updated Hojo mon colour.
- Greatly expanded the Batak surname list (using tribe names).

# Event modifiers
- The LNY dragon boon related to health should now increse your health rather than massively increasing your fertility and having no effect on your health.
- Made the prestige penalty for failing to overthrow your Permanent Regent much harsher.
- Resigning from the council due to not wanting to support the Grand Chancellor/a prospective Grand Chancellor now hurts your monthly prestige a bit more.
- Being forced to adopt a voting pattern by the Grand Chancellor now hurts your monthly prestige a bit more.
- A forced actual regency now lowers defensive plot power, plot discovery chance, and arrest chance. Yes, this means it's an excellent chance to literally stab the Tenno/etc. in the back if he has been getting uppity.
- The MoH now impacts defensive plot power, plot discovery chance, and arrest chance if it's not at Average, being higher with higher MoH and lower with lower. Intersting times should therefore be a bit more interesting.
- Lowered revolt risk increase at Poor and Lost MoH rating; it's more fun if the issues come from existing characters, after all...

# Events
- Many events that previously discriminated against women are now much more open if women are viewed as equal or superior.
- Many event-spawned characters' gender now depends on how genders are viewed in the appropriate context.
- Fixed a bunch of tumbling-related issues; vanilla sure is the gift that keeps giving in that regard...
- Dharmics now only spawn with caste traits where sensible.
- Most trait gains and losses now use the relevant scripted_effects, resulting in notifications when there's randomness and other niftiness.
- Fixed many, many orientation-related issues.
- Several events with implicit tumbling (based off of the event text) now actually come with tumbling.
- Tweaked Iyomante rewards and logic slightly.
- Many events that only could involve your primary wife can now involve other wives if you have polygamy. For example, a secondary wife can catch you with a lover, meaning that imprisoning your primary wife doesn't allow for easy infidelity.
- Chinese Imperial emperors, Shoguns, and the Tenno should no longer get the ERE eunuch events (the ones that care about eunuchs have their own stuff).
- Fixed some instances where unreformed Tianxia pagans had access to religious features they shouldn't have.
- Northern Court emperors should no longer get renamed if they depose the Southern Court.
- Guests can now get a hangover at the Iyomante even with a middling amount of sake.
- Dharmics that are unlikely to possess elepants no longer gift elepants as part of the Business focus trade route events.
- Guests trying to set up a Business Focus trade route no longer have the option to gift elephants, warriors, or camels; everyone instead gets horses, seeing as those are all but omnipresent and generally are logical.
- Slightly changed Court Mu and Gut event rewards and logic.
- Gim Daeseong should no longer get an event talking about himself in third person if he goes to Bulguksa.
- Slightly adjusted Bulguksa/Seokguram pilgrimage logic and rewards.
- Adjusted Chuseok logic and rewards slightly, and added an option to potentially convert to Confucian.
- Split Hwarang events into multiple files, re-indexed them, and made assorted minor improvements.
- Started replacing add_/remove_trait with add_/remove_trait_<trait>_effect inside assorted random blocks.
- Skills now matter quite a bit when giving Spiritual Guidance.
- Adjusted piety/prestige gain/loss for various Spiritual Guidance events, and added more instances of gain/loss.
- Paranoid characters receiving Spiritual Guidance to become more Trusting now actually move towards Trusting rather than towards Just.
- You are no longer notified if the person giving you Spiritual Guidance causes you to lose the trait they're supposedly teaching you about, seeing as that'd spoil things.

# Factions
- Made it impossible to scheme/use your spymaster to force the Permanent Regent's dynasty members out of the Regency Loyalists faction.
- Made it impossible to block the Tenno from starting or remaining in the faction to depose the Shogun using scheming/spymasters.
- None of the Japanese factions (for becoming the permanent regent (either kind), being a regency loyalist (either kind), becoming the shogun, or ousting the shogun) are now blocked for council members.
- The faction to become the permanent regent now requires the prospective faction leader to be a king, be a duke with 1000 prestige and a regency bloodline, or be a duke with 5000 prestige (comparable to the Shogunate faction's conditions) unless the liege already has a non-permanent regent. The faction leader gets to stay in charge if the regency ends.
- Made the AI less willing to start a faction to give the Shogun a Permanent Regent unless they have an active Regency bloodline, and also made it less willing to back such a faction unless the faction leader has such a bloodline, seeing as the Ashikaga Shoguns shouldn't have to be overly concerned about this threat. This does not apply if the Shogun already has a regent.
- Updated relevant faction logic to let unreformed/sensibly reformed Ryukyuans join the fun that is the Japanese government faction mess. A Tenno knockoff cannot join the fun, but he can be the target of some of the fun.
- Vassals that'd hypothetically be eligible for Chinese Imperial will now not start factions to put women on the throne of a Chinese Imperial empire if the title is Agnatic unless they happen to be female, and will also be unwilling to back female claimants. You're supposed to faction for their male children; silly AI!
- It is no longer possible to start or join claimant factions against the Tenno if you are someone that cares about the Tenno, or to start them on behalf of someone that cares about the Tenno... unless it's for some kingdom (other than k_chrysanthemum_throne) he holds and he also holds an emperor-tier title. In that case, still no claiming it for members of the Tenno's dynasty. If you are someone that cares about the Tenno, you'll have to use the Shogunate faction if you want to take his empire title (or you can become the Permanent Regent, if you prefer a less direct approach) and you're not from his dynasty. If you're from his dynasty, well, see below.
- Made the AI very unwilling to support claimants that'd switch the realm from assorted eastern governments to other kinds of feudalism if they can have one of those assorted governments, particularly within subgroups (e.g. a character with Japanese Bureaucracy will be more concerned about a Japanese Feudal liege being replaced by someone that'd have regular Feudal than they'd be about a Chinese Imperial liege being replaced by a regular Feudal liege), as well as the opposite way around.
- Chinese and Japanese viceroys no longer consider "I am a viceroy!" a reason to be more eager to seek independence if their liege has a government they approve of (Chinese Imperial in the former case, Japanese/Divine Imperial in the latter).
- The AI should now be considerably less likely to seek independence if their liege has a Permanent Regent if it cares about such things and is a de jure vassal.
- AI Japanese Bureaucracy/Feudal/Monastic Feudal de jure vassals should not seek independence from a Shogun if they instead could restore the Tenno's rule.
- AI Japanese Bureaucracy/Feudal/Monastic Feudal de jure vassals should not seek independence from the Tenno if they could become the Shogun.
- AI Confucian Bureaucracy viceroys are now less likely to seek independence under a Chinese Imperial liege unless the Mandate rating of the liege drops below Average unless they also have non-viceroy titles of the same tier.
- The AI Tenno shouldn't ever seek independence if under a Japanese Bureaucracy/Feudal/Monastic Feudal liege; overthrowing the Shogunate is obviously better.
- If the Mandate rating of a liege drops to Lost
- Added new faction to press de facto claims of valid Imperial Family members on the Chrysanthemum Throne.
- The Tenno is no longer blocked from the End Shogunate faction by having a regent, but an actual regency is still a blocker.
- The AI is now more likely to join the Regency Loyalists if the Permanent Regent has managed to shift the Regency Power laws far in his favour. Confucian Bureaucracy vassals will become more eager to seek independence.
- Further lowered Confucian Bureaucracy Independence faction willingness against someone with a positive MoH.
- The "The Three Mountains" Doctrine now makes co-religionist vassals much more likely to seek independence.
- The "The Three Mountains" Doctrine now makes co-religionist vassals much more likely to start/join factions for their own claims.
- Vassals following a religion with the "The Three Mountains" Doctrine that have a liege with the same religion can now start and join factions even if on the council.
- The Grand Chancellor can now join the Claimant, Independence, and Takeover factions even if on the council.
- Non-Imperial Family WotRS members are now more likely to start and join the Shogunate faction, though the Master is less likely to support someone else's attempt to become the Shogun.
- Non-Imperial Family WotRS members are now less likely to start and join the End Shogunate faction, though the Master is more likely to help destroy it so that they can create a new, improved Shogunate of their own.
- The Powerful Vassal Takeover Faction now results in abdication to the faction leader rather than the most liked vassal if both the liege and the vassal has a Chinese- or Japanese-style government (Note: As in vanilla, this faction is not available against rel heads).
- The Grand Chancellor is now more likely to start the Takeover faction.
- Significantly increased the AI opinion thresholds for leaving various Japanese factions, and increased the severity of low opinion on the AI's willingness to start/join those factions.
- Significantly reduced the prestige requirement for starting the Permanent Regency factions.
- Made the AI care less about the opinion of their liege for the Takeover Faction if the liege has a sub-Average MoH rating.
- Made AI Grand Chancellors willing to join the Takeover Faction against their liege at significantly higher opinion.
- The AI is no longer more eager to faction against their own dynasty just because the dynasty has a sub-Average MoH rating; obviously, you want to overthrow the ruler, not the dynasty!
- Told the AI that revolting to put a member of their Chinese Imperial liege's dynasty on the Dragon Throne (or pretender knockoff) is perhaps not something it should try to do unless from the same dynasty; you want to overthrow the dynasty, not the ruler!
- Told AI ConBu rulers not to try to seek independence fromn a CI liege from the same dynasty. Just overthrow them!
- Told AI ConBu rulers that overthrowing their liege's CI dynasty probably is better than seeking independence if there's already a faction seeking to overthrow the ruler.
- Told AI ConBu rulers of the same dynasty and culture as a CI/ConBu/EI liege to maybe not seek independence so often. Take over the realm from within!
- Told AI JI/DI/JBu/JFeudal/JMonFeudal rulers of the same dynasty and culture as a JI/DI/JBu/JFeudal/JMonFeudal liege to maybe not seek independence so often. Take over the realm from within!
- Told AI dynasty members of CI/ConBu/EI or JI/DI/JBu/JFeudal/JMonFeudal from the same subset to not support Takeover factions led by other dynasties very often, seeing as that'd result in their dynasty losing power. Obviously, you should be the new ruler!
- Told the AI not to support non-dynastic Former Sons/Daughters of Heaven leading Claimant or Takeover factions as often.
- The AI will no longer back the Takeover faction against a CI/ConBu/EI liege if it's eligible for one of those governments and has a claim, seeing at should want to be the new ruler.
- Tightened Independence faction requirements for people that should care about the Tenno or the knockoff.
- Non-Japanese Feudal AIs should now be less into the creation of Shogunates.
- Told the AI that the Imperial Claimant faction is maybe not something to start quite so frequently if you're not the Imperial Claimant in question.
- Increased Claimant Faction chance against CI rulers that are perceived as tyrants.
- Vassals of a CI liege are now only allowed to start the Takeover faction if one of the following is true: You are the Grand Chancellor and your influence is Overwhelming, you are a Powerful Vassal and your influence is Overwhelming, or your liege has Lost the MoH. This should make Poor MoH more likely to be "Vassals fight each other while trying to become strong enough to make a play for the Dragon Throne (or pretender equivalent)" as opposed to "Vassals quickly make a play for the Dragon Throne (or pretender equivalent), making the Pacification CB effectively irrelevant".
- The Independence faction is no longer unlocked for all vassals of CI realms at Poor MoH, though the restrictions on councillors joining the faction are revoked at that point. The goal is to promote infighting rather than disintegration at this stage.
- Rulers that are mourning can no longer be part of any factions.
- Extended the independence faction logic for k_orthodox to k_hellenic_pagan (provided it is a theocracy), meaning the Pontifex Maximus won't be ungrateful and turn against the Augustus.

# Focuses
- Adjusted AI focus logic slightly for a few of our governments.
- The AI is now more likely to pick non-martial education focuses if a bureaucracy.
- Adjusted AI childhood focus logic for bureaucracies.
- Made the AI smarter about the Faith and Heritage focuses and Chinese/Japanese government eligibility.
- AI rulers with a Chinese/Japanese government will now be less likely to pick the Business focus. Merchants are lowly people that don't contribute to society, after all.
- The Ryukyuan knockoff and their Permanent Regent is now as likely to pick the Intrigue focus as the Tenno/etc.
- Koshin characters are now a bit more likely to pick the Carousing focus.
- Celibate mourners are no longer allowed to use the Seduction focus.

# Game rules
- Deleted the culture game rule for Chinese Imperial, seeing as it was causing way too many problems. It will NOT be returning.
- Removed the game rule related to the "Confucian Principles" Doctrine since the requirements for the required religion have changed considerably and said Doctrine no longer exists.
- Removed the game rule to disable the Mandate of Heaven; it's very integrated into lots of stuff now.
- Added a game rule for Chinese implosions.
- Cleaned up Liao settling game rule.
- Cleaned up Liao event troops game rule.
- Added a game rule to give pagan religions a starting MA boost.
- Added a game rule to restrict the availability of the Pagan County Conquest CB.
- Added a game rule to make the Pagan County Conquest CB available against overseas targets if a non-Peaceful Seafarer.
- Added a game rule to restrict the valid targets for the Pagan Subjugation CB.
- Added a game rule to restrict the range of the Pagan Subjugation CB.
- Added a game rule to let pagans start their GHWs before the Crusades and/or Jihads start.
- Added a game rule to give pagans the opportunity to feudalize (or become an MR, if you insist on using that hardcoded mess...) prior to reformation.
- Removed duchy and kingdom creation restriction game rules; they're mostly controlled by Chinese laws now.
- Added a game rule to disable the Nomad Subjugation CB, seeing as it's a balance nightmare.
- Added a Holy War range game rule, potentially preventing early ovearses holy wars.
- Added an Interesting Muslims game rule, making it possible to disable those due to their relation to Sacred Timeline history.
- Added a Manifest Destiny game rule, making it possible to disable the Manifest Destiny Invasion CB as it is railroady. Perhaps Seljuk and Timur should get Tribal invasion from their bloodlines...?
- Added a game rule to limit Indian Subjugation targets by culture/religion.
- Added a game rule to limit Indian Subjugation range.

# Governments
- Removed inherent monthly piety from Chinese Imperial, Japanese Imperial, and Japanese Monastic Feudal.
- Lowered the amount of concubines for Chinese Imperial and Japanese Imperial to 100.
- Lowered the extra court size for Chinese Imperial and Japanese Imperial to 500.
- Dynasty members of Chinese Imperial emperors no longer get Chinese Imperial (unless they happen to control another Chinese Imperial empire).
- Overhauled Chinese Imperial eligibility.
- Japanese Imperial no longer accepts any liege governments, which should make the Tenno a bit less likely to decide that this Shogunate thing isn't so bad...
- The Tenno can no longer freely revoke duchies, making it harder to deal with duke vassals that swap to Japanese Feudal or Japanese Monastic Feudal.
- Added Eastern Imperial government.
- Overhauled Confucian Bureaucracy (chinese_vassal_government) eligibility.
- Added Japanese Bureaucracy government.
- Overhauled Japanese Feudal eligibility.
- Overhauled Japanese Monastic Feudal eligibility.
- Added Divine Imperial government.
- Changed government colours for Chinese Imperial and Nomadic.
- Updated the colour of the Japanese Imperial and Japanese Feudal governments to better match the colours of the icons/frames.
- Imperial (roman_imperial_government) is no longer an option for anyone that'd be eligible for any of the assorted eastern governments. This prevents certain weird scenarios that'd potentially cause a government switch, e.g. a character eligible for Japanese Bureaucracy being Imperial one moment and losing it the next because a vassal happened to inherit the Chrysanthemum Throne.
- Chinese, Eastern, Japanese, and Divine Imperial now have an inherent +10 vassal opinion to balance out the -10 vassal opinion they suffer from being locked into Imperial Administration.
- Unreformed and Reformed Bön now gives you Monastic Feudal regardless of culture, which should make the religion a bit more interesting with unconventional cultures.
- Bamar culture no longer unlocks Monatic Feudal for Buddhists, seeing as that goverment isn't a great fit for Bagan/etc.
- The Confucian and Japanese Bureaucracy goverments no longer have cities as preferred holdings (they are still allowed to hold them without penalties) to hopefully keep the AI from doing some stupid things.
- The various Chinese and Japanese governments can no longer hold tribal holdings without penalty even with matching culture.
- Japanese Feudal now gets opinion penalties for raised levies.
- Japanese Monastic Feudal no longer get to freely revoke titles from lots of governments.
- Removed the ability to freely revoke titles from wrong religion tribal rulers for all Chinese- and Japanese-style governments.
- Characters eligible for Chinese Imperial, Confucian Bureaucracy, Eastern Imperial, Japanese Imperial, Japanese Bureaucracy, and Divine Imperial should now be less likely to randomly become republics, MR or otherwise.
- Characters eligible for Monastic Feudal, Japanese Imperial, Japanese Monastic Feudal, and Divine Imperial shouldnow be less likely to randomly become theocracies.
- The Sapa Inca can now technically be tribal without HF.
- Chinese Imperial rulers no longer allow matrilineal marriages if Agnatic, which should help mitigate some AI stupidity. Considering Imperial Marriages are a thing, they don't really benefit from having them available if China (and if not China, they obviously want to be China).

# Grace system
- Grace penalties now only check for the EoC and his close dynastic relatives (and his dynastic heir and/or dynastic children, in a handful of places), which should help with performance since the third (and most expensive) any_dynasty_member check now is gone in a lot of places.
- Several things that previously resulted in Grace penalties no longer matter. Yes, the EoC might be unhappy if you e.g. break up with him, but as far as China's government is concerned you're not in the wrong.
- General: Vassals of China can now use a number of Grace interactions.
- General: Pretender tributaries can no longer use the Grace system.
- General: Aside from vassals and below of China, the Grace system is now restricted to independent rulers, king-tier vassals, Grand Chancellors, Permanent Regents, and powerful vassals of independent rulers. China does not concern itself with barbarian nobodies.
- General: The EoC can no longer accept Tributes or grant Boons if inaccessible or incapable.
- General: If you are inaccessible or have a regent (including a Permanent Regent) you are now unable to use the Grace system.
- General: The game rule for disabling the Grace system is no longer available. The game rule for Imperial Marriages remains.
- General: The EoC can no longer enable or disable specific Tributes or Boons. They can still be refused, but that comes with an MoH penalty.
- General: The AI now handles Grace decisions with offset on_x_pulse events, improving performance.
- General: The Chinese influence group laws now impact what happens when a Tribute arrives or a Boon is requested, potentially making things less nice for the EoC and more nice for others.
- General: Various logic overhauls.
- General: Cleaned up irrelevant logic.
- General: The EoC's Mandate rating now affects the Grace gained for different Tributes; if the Mandate rating is high, the Grace gain is lower, while if the Mandate rating is low the Grace gain is higher.
- General: The AI is now more likely to offer more powerful Tributes if the EoC's Mandate rating is higher.
- General: Characters sent to China will no longer accept court invites. You thought you were so clever...
- General: The EoC re-gifting a Tribute now gives the full amount of Grace.
- General: Only half your Grace (if positive; it's already only 10 % if negative) can be inherited.
- General: Pretender emperors no longer lose most of their negative Grace on inheritance.
- General: Vassals of China now receive some Grace (scaled by tier) on_yearly_pulse.
- General: Rebalanced yearly Grace income for tributaries; title tier now matters quite a bit.
- General: Grace loss for usurpation now scales with title tier; emperor/king/rel head title > non-rel head duke title > county, baronies don't count.
- Send Gift: Is now available for vassals and below of China.
- Send Gift: Now costs 500 gold or 1 year of income, whichever is larger.
- Become Tributary: Rel heads can no longer become tributaries by choice.
- Become Tributary: Top lieges of the Tenno or the Ryukyuan knockoff that should care can no longer become tributaries by choice.
- Offer Eunuch: Is now available for vassals and below of China.
- Offer Eunuch: If you are someone that should care, you cannot send members of the Imperial Family or the family of the Ryukyuan knockoff.
- Offer Eunuch: You can no longer send the EoC's dynasty members as eunuchs. China would certainly not approve.
- Offer Eunuch: "Loyal Servant" eunuchs gotten from the eunuch merchant can now be sent to China. They do of course remain loyal servants of the sender...
- Offer Eunuch: Your own unlanded prisoners can now be sent as eunuchs to China.
- Offer Eunuch: You can now send pretty terrible eunuchs to China even if they are not from your dynasty, though the Grace you get for them will probably leave something to be desired.
- Offer Eunuch: China will no longer re-gift eunuchs.
- Offer Concubine: Is now available for vassals and below of China.
- Offer Concubine: If you are someone that should care, you cannot send members of the Imperial Family or the family of the Ryukyuan knockoff.
- Offer Concubine: You can no longer send siblings, ancestors, or descendants of the EoC as concubines.
- Offer Concubine: Beautiful women can now be sent even if they're not related to anyone powerful.
- Offer Concubine: It's now possible to send your own lover to the EoC. She may or may not break up with you, however.
- Offer Concubine: The concubine may also break up with other lovers.
- Offer Commander: Is now available for vassals and below of China.
- Offer Commander: Event-spawned female commanders can now be sent to China.
- Offer Commander: Worse commanders can now be sent.
- Offer Physician: Is now available for vassals and below of China.
- Offer Physician: Foreigners can no longer send a physician if they have any disease in their realm. China would probably suspect foul play.
- Offer Physician: Vassals and below cannot send a physician if they have a disease in their demesne. China would probably suspect foul play.
- Relief Expedition: Removed. It worked somewhat weirdly, and there are more interesting things to do on a similar theme.
- Offer Artifact: Is now available for vassals and below of China.
- Offer Artifact: Extended the protection on the Imperial Regalia to all characters that should care.
- Offer Artifact: If the EoC is a member of the Hermetics, you can now send ingredients as Tribute.
- Offer Artifact: If you have a claim on China you can of course not send the Heirloom Seal of the Realm to the EoC; it clearly belongs to you!
- Hand Over Title: New Tribute, only available for vassals or below of China. Allows handing over a (non rel head non-de jure) county to the EoC, in exchange for Grace.
- Hand Over Title: The EoC might re-gift the title to a feudal vassal or a courtier.
- Renounce Claims (Tribute): New Tribute. Renounces ALL claims on titles in de facto China (if independent) or those held by the EoC (if a vassal or below of China) in exchange for Grace.
- Cancel Favor (Tribute): New Tribute, requiring Conclave (+ JD). Offer the EoC the opportunity to get out of owing you a Favor, in exchange for Grace.
- Cancel Favor: The AI will always accept -- and will always recommend accepting, if the GC -- this Tribute.
- Transfer Prosperity: New Tribute, requiring RD (+ JD), only available for vassals or below of China. Transfers one prosperity level from a random (ideally non-capital) demesne province to the EoC's choice of his capital (if not Booming), a random demesne province (if any are not Booming, preferring Depopulated provinces), or a random (preferably non-tribal) realm province not in the sender's demesne.
- Transfer Society Currency: New Tribute, available if in the same society as the EoC. Transfer 1000 society currency to the EoC in exchange for Grace.
- Transfer Society Currency: If the society is a secret society the GC only gets to make a recommendation if part of the same secret society.
- Boost Mandate: New Tribute, available for vassals and below of China. Spend 1000 prestige or 1 year of prestige income, whichever is larger, to boost the EoC's Mandate.
- Increase Tributary tier: Rel heads can no longer make this offer, nor can the Shogun.
- Offer Border Adjustment: New Tribute, available for independent rulers. Offer China a duchy in de jure or de facto China in exchange for Grace and a 10 year truce. Not available for rel heads or the Shogun.
- Request Truce: New Boon, available for independent rulers being attacked by China. Invalidates the relevant war and grants a bidirectional 10 year truce.
- Request Chinese Peace Deal: Increased cost slightly.
- Request Chinese Peace Deal: Tributaries of China can no longer request a Peace Deal.
- Request Chinese Peace Deal: The duration of the peace deal is now 25 years (was 50).
- Request Trade Contract: Removed; it got weird with China both being unable to benefit and possibly being in a poor position to influence the Silk Road.
- Request Favor: New Boon, available for vassals and below of China that neither owe a Favor to the EoC nor are owed one from the EoC. Spend Grace to request a Favor.
- Cancel Favor (Boon): New Boon, available for vassals and below of China that owe a Favor to the EoC. Spend Grace to request that the EoC forgets all about the owed Favor.
- Request County: New Boon, available for feudal (is_feudal = yes) vassals and below of China. Spend Grace to request that the EoC gives you another county. The EoC will potentially give you one from a vassal or below instead, if any have multiple valid counties and vassals are not too powerful. The EoC will be expected to compensate the vassal in some manner.
- Request County: The EoC's GC pays less Grace.
- Request County for Other: New Boon, available for feudal (is_feudal = yes) vassals and below of China. Spend Grace to request that the EoC gives a county to an unlanded kinsman/close relative/friend/lover of yours. The EoC will potentially give them one from a vassal or below instead (possibly even one of your counties), if any have multiple valid counties and vassals are not too powerful. The EoC will be expected to compensate the vassal in some manner.
- Request County for Other: The EoC's GC pays less Grace.
- Request Imperial Marriage: Cost increased.
- Request Imperial Marriage: Republics and theocracies need not apply.
- Request Imperial Marriage: Vassals and below of China now use a Grace-based decision rather than a gold-based decision.
- Request Imperial Marriage: The EoC can now potentially opt to send an interesting non-Imperial bride.
- Request Imperial Marriage: The EoC's GC pays less Grace.
- Request Imperial Marriage: Any resulting peace deal now lasts 25 years (was 50).
- Request Imperial Marriage: The EoC is now permitted to select less good kinswomen (e.g. ones that have been maimed) to marry off; it's still a great honour, right?
- Request Chinese Physician: Can now be used by vassals and below of China.
- Request Chinese Physician: Cannot be refused for vassals.
- Request Siege Engineers: Can now be used by vassals and below of China.
- Request Siege Engineer: Cannot be refused for vassals.
- Request Strategist: Can now be used by vassals and below of China.
- Request Strategist: Cost increased.
- Request Strategist: Cannot be refused for vassals.
- Request Scholar-Bureaucrat: Can now be used by vassals and below of China.
- Request Scholar-Bureaucrat: Cannot be refused for vassals.
- Request Master Engineer: Can now be used by vassals and below of China.
- Request Master Engineer: Cannot be refused for vassals.
- Request Border Adjustment: Cost significantly increased.
- Dismiss Councillor: New Boon, available for vassals and below of China. Spend Grace to request that the EoC fires a specific councillor.
- Dismiss Councillor: Cost depends on the target, and is lower for the GC.
- Dismiss Councillor: If the EoC's GC is fully empowered this Boon cannot be refused if said GC requests it.
- Become Grand Chancellor: New Boon, available for feudal (is_feudal = yes) vassals of China that have the relevant ambition. Spend Grace to bypass the "Get councillors to support me" part of the ambition. This Boon cannot be refused by the EoC.
- Request Command: New Boon, available for vassals of China. Request to be given a Commander position. Commanders appointed in this fashion cannot be relieved of command for 10 years.
- Renounce Claims (Boon): New Boon, available for vassals and below of China. Spend Grace to demand that the EoC renounces any current claims on your titles.
- Renounce Claims (Boon): The EoC's GC pays less Grace.
- Renounce Claims (Boon): If the EoC's GC is fully empowered this Boon cannot be refused if said GC requests it.
- Renounce Claims (Boon): If the asker is a powerful vassal, this Boon cannot be refused if vassals have sufficient authority.
- Renounce Claims (Boon): This Boon cannot be refused if vassals are fully empowered.
- Request Imperial Praise: New Boon, available for vassals and below of China. Spend Grace to get your vassals, the EoC's vassals, and the EoC's councillors to like you more for five years.
- Request Imperial Praise: The EoC's GC pays less Grace.
- Request Imperial Praise: If the EoC's GC is fully empowered this Boon cannot be refused if said GC requests it.
- Request Imperial Praise: If the asker is a powerful vassal, this Boon cannot be refused if vassals have sufficient authority.
- Request Imperial Praise: This Boon cannot be refused if vassals are fully empowered.
- Ask to Decrease Tributary Tier: Cost significantly increased for Client States and Protectorates, particularly ones China is interested in keeping.
- Request Chinese Artifact: Can now be used by vassals and below of China.
- Request Chinese Artifact: Cannot be refused for vassals.
- Request Chinese Artifact: Cost slightly increased.
- Request Chinese Artifact: The EoC no longer gets to pick which artifact to send (mostly to reduce player tedium).
- Request Chinese Artifact: The EoC now pays a flat gold cost, instead of a cost scaling with the artifact tier.
- Request Chinese Artifact: The quality of the artifact will now tend to be higher if the EoC's Mandate rating is higher.
- Request Chinese Artifact: Plenty of new options have been added, primarily books.
- Bestow Grace: The EoC can now bestow Grace on their vassals and below.
- Non-eunuchs sent by vassals and below of China are no longer disinherited, divorced, or forced to lose all their consorts.
- Concubines sent to China no longer break up with the person they're given to.
- Concubines sent by vassals and below of China no longer have a chance of losing their lovers.
- Chinese Princesses no longer break up with their lovers if they marry their lover.
- Chinese Princesses married to a vassal or below of China no longer have a chance of losing their lovers.
- China will no longer send master engineers or scholar bureaucrats to live with tribal or nomadic characters.
- The AI will now prefer to send the Heirloom Seal of the Realm to China... if they shouldn't try to hang on to it.
- Positive Grace is now divided by 5 on inheritance, keeping Grace inflation down.
- If the recipient of a lesser bride decides they are not fine with the boon, their opinion of the EoC is lowered.
- Breaking a Chinese Peace Deal now gives you a two year truce with the other party, much like if you broke an NAP.
- Chinese Princesses now need to make up their minds as to which trait they're supposed to be having.

- Modified the bloodline interface to make it possible to have a longer desc.
- Improved event picture setup.
